================================================================================
SECTION 1: APPLICATION OVERVIEW & ARCHITECTURAL SUMMARY
================================================================================
Keepsake is an immersive 3D interactive web experience where players explore a low-poly
garden of memories. Built with modern React 19, Three.js, React Three Fiber, and Zustand,
the application combines procedural environmental generation, custom low-poly art, diegetic
parchment UI, and interactive storytelling.

Key Architecture Pillars:
  1. 3D WebGL Scene & Pipeline:
     - Powered by Three.js and @react-three/fiber with @react-three/drei.
     - Dynamic directional lighting, warm sunlight, gentle moonlight, and hemisphere sky fill.
  2. Procedural Infinite Low-Poly Terrain:
     - Chunked terrain system generating deterministic low-poly terrain tiles on the fly.
     - Height calculations driven by customized Simplex noise math and elevation clamping.
     - Procedural distribution of low-poly trees, stones, flowers, and atmospheric dust motes.
  3. Explorer Character Controller & Camera Rig:
     - Custom procedural low-poly 3D explorer mesh (hat, coat, backpack, lantern, animated limbs).
     - Responsive WASD / Arrow movement physics, sprint (Shift), jump (Space), and gravity.
     - Smooth orbital third-person camera with mouse drag/pointer-lock and obstacle avoidance.
  4. Interactive Memory Monuments & Radiance:
     - 3D stone pedestals holding glowing, levitating memory crystals.
     - MemoryRadiance volumetric light motes, nearest-memory telemetry calculation, and proximity detection.
     - Discovered memories trigger journal entries, sound cues, and visual fanfare.
  5. Procedural 3D Asset Library & Structures:
     - Storybook cottages, red torii gates, curved & straight bridges, lanterns, noticeboards, campsites.
     - GLB/GLTF runtime extraction and exporter systems.
  6. Diegetic UI & Atmospheric Experience:
     - Ink-wash calligraphic loading screen with story prologue.
     - Parchment-themed HUD with compass heading, memory discovery counter, and journal modal.
     - Custom botanical SVG pointer cursor that smoothly follows user interaction.
  7. Centralized Reactive State:
     - Zustand store managing player coordinate stream, discovered memory registry, audio levels,
       and active modal dialogues.
================================================================================

================================================================================
SECTION 2: PROJECT DEPENDENCIES & RUNTIME SPECIFICATIONS
================================================================================
The project utilizes modern npm packages managed via package.json.

--- [A] PRODUCTION RUNTIME DEPENDENCIES (Core Frameworks & Libraries) ---

  * @react-three/drei (^10.7.9)
    Description: Curated collection of useful helper components, gizmos, and shaders for React Three Fiber.

  * @react-three/fiber (^9.8.2)
    Description: Declarative React renderer for Three.js, bridging React state with 3D scene graphs.

  * lucide-react (^1.70.0)
    Description: Clean, customizable modern SVG icon library for HUD controls, badges, and navigation.

  * react (^19.3.1)
    Description: Core UI component framework (version 19) providing concurrent rendering and hooks.

  * react-dom (^19.3.1)
    Description: React rendering engine for web browsers and DOM hierarchy manipulation.

  * three (^0.187.0)
    Description: Underlying 3D computer graphics WebGL library providing scenes, geometries, and materials.

  * zustand (^5.1.0)
    Description: High-performance, lightweight client-side state store for game states and player coordinates.

--- [B] DEVELOPMENT & BUILD DEPENDENCIES (Tooling, Types & Linters) ---

  * @eslint/js (^10.0.1)
    Description: Official ESLint JavaScript rule configurations.

  * @types/node (^24.19.2)
    Description: TypeScript type definitions for Node.js APIs.

  * @types/react (^19.3.0)
    Description: TypeScript type definitions for React 19.

  * @types/react-dom (^19.3.0)
    Description: TypeScript type definitions for React DOM.

  * @types/three (^0.187.0)
    Description: TypeScript type definitions for Three.js classes, geometries, and mathematical primitives.

  * @vitejs/plugin-react (^6.1.2)
    Description: Official Vite plugin enabling React fast refresh and JSX transformation.

  * eslint (^10.14.0)
    Description: Pluggable JavaScript/TypeScript linter detecting code quality issues.

  * eslint-plugin-react-hooks (^7.1.1)
    Description: ESLint rules verifying correct adherence to React Hook rules.

  * eslint-plugin-react-refresh (^0.5.7)
    Description: ESLint rules validating React components for fast refresh compatibility.

  * globals (^17.13.0)
    Description: Global variables dictionary for ESLint environments.

  * typescript (~6.0.3)
    Description: Static type checker and compiler enforcing type safety across all components and math.

  * typescript-eslint (^8.72.1)
    Description: Tooling that enables ESLint to parse and check TypeScript code.

  * vite (^8.3.6)
    Description: Next-generation frontend dev server and production bundler offering instantaneous HMR.

--- [C] NPM SCRIPTS & CLI COMMANDS ---

  * npm run dev
    Command: vite
    Action : Starts the local Vite development server with Hot Module Replacement.

  * npm run build
    Command: tsc -b && vite build
    Action : Runs TypeScript type checking (tsc -b) and compiles production assets via Vite.

  * npm run lint
    Command: eslint .
    Action : Scans the entire codebase for linting and style errors using ESLint.

  * npm run preview
    Command: vite preview
    Action : Serves the locally compiled production build for testing.

# React + TypeScript + Vite

This template provides a minimal setup to get React working in Vite with HMR and some ESLint rules.

Currently, two official plugins are available:

- [@vitejs/plugin-react](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react) uses [Oxc](https://oxc.rs)
- [@vitejs/plugin-react-swc](https://github.com/vitejs/vite-plugin-react/blob/main/packages/plugin-react-swc) uses [SWC](https://swc.rs/)

## React Compiler

The React Compiler is not enabled on this template because of its impact on dev & build performances. To add it, see [this documentation](https://react.dev/learn/react-compiler/installation).

## Expanding the ESLint configuration

If you are developing a production application, we recommend updating the configuration to enable type-aware lint rules:

```js
export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...

      // Remove tseslint.configs.recommended and replace with this
      tseslint.configs.recommendedTypeChecked,
      // Alternatively, use this for stricter rules
      tseslint.configs.strictTypeChecked,
      // Optionally, add this for stylistic rules
      tseslint.configs.stylisticTypeChecked,

      // Other configs...
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

You can also install [eslint-plugin-react-x](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-x) and [eslint-plugin-react-dom](https://npmx.dev/package/eslint-plugin-react-dom) for React-specific lint rules:

```js
// eslint.config.js
import reactX from 'eslint-plugin-react-x'
import reactDom from 'eslint-plugin-react-dom'

export default defineConfig([
  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{ts,tsx}'],
    extends: [
      // Other configs...
      // Enable lint rules for React
      reactX.configs['recommended-typescript'],
      // Enable lint rules for React DOM
      reactDom.configs.recommended,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      parserOptions: {
        project: ['./tsconfig.node.json', './tsconfig.app.json'],
        tsconfigRootDir: import.meta.dirname,
      },
      // other options...
    },
  },
])

```

// src/App.tsx
import React from 'react';
import { GardenScene } from './scenes/GardenScene/GardenScene';
import { LoadingScreen } from './components/LoadingScreen/LoadingScreen';
import { GameHUD } from './components/UI/GameHUD';
import { CustomCursor } from './components/UI/CustomCursor';

export const App: React.FC = () => {
  return (
    <main className="app-container">
      {/* Custom Botanical SVG Pointer (Active on Loading Screen, Modals, and while holding Alt) */}
      <CustomCursor />

      {/* Calligraphic Brushstroke Loading & Intro Screen */}
      <LoadingScreen />

      {/* Infinite Chunk-Based 3D Low-Poly World, Explorer Character & Pointer-Lock Camera */}
      <GardenScene />

      {/* Parchment Exploration HUD & Memory Inspection Modal */}
      <GameHUD />
    </main>
  );
};

export default App;

FILE #16 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/store/useGameStore.ts
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\store\useGameStore.ts
FILE TYPE     : .ts
DESCRIPTION   : Global Zustand game store managing player coords, active memory, discovered memories, and audio settings.
================================================================================
// src/store/useGameStore.ts
import { create } from 'zustand';
import type {
    EnvironmentLandmarkZone,
    MemoryItem,
} from '../systems/MemoryManager/memoryTypes';

export type AppPhase = 'loading' | 'intro' | 'exploring';
export type IntroStage = 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6;
export type QualityPreset = 'high' | 'low';
export type CharacterArchetype = 'female' | 'male';
export type ActiveModal =
    | 'none'
    | 'memory'
    | 'journal'
    | 'friends'
    | 'settings'
    | 'createMemory';

export type InputMode = 'camera' | 'cursor';

export const playerTelemetry = {
    position: [0, 0, 7.5] as [number, number, number],
    velocity: [0, 0, 0] as [number, number, number],
    rotationY: Math.PI,
    cameraYaw: 0,
    cameraPitch: 0.22,
    cameraDistance: 5.2,
    isGrounded: true,
    moveState: 'idle' as 'idle' | 'walk' | 'run' | 'jump',
    nearestMemoryDistance: 999,
    nearestMemoryPosition: [0, 0, 0] as [number, number, number],
    nearestMemoryRadiance: 0, // 0 (normal muted world) -> 1 (full golden vibrancy)
};

interface GameState {
    phase: AppPhase;
    setPhase: (phase: AppPhase) => void;

    introStage: IntroStage;
    setIntroStage: (stage: IntroStage) => void;

    quality: QualityPreset;
    toggleQuality: () => void;

    // Input Mode FSM (Mutually exclusive: camera mode vs cursor mode)
    inputMode: InputMode;
    setInputMode: (mode: InputMode) => void;
    toggleInputMode: () => void;

    // Character selection
    characterModel: CharacterArchetype;
    setCharacterModel: (model: CharacterArchetype) => void;
    toggleCharacterModel: () => void;

    // Terrain generation & ready state
    isInitialTerrainReady: boolean;
    setInitialTerrainReady: (ready: boolean) => void;
    activeChunkCoord: [number, number];
    setActiveChunkCoord: (coord: [number, number]) => void;

    // Persistent memory storage
    persistentMemories: MemoryItem[];
    registerPersistentMemories: (memories: MemoryItem[]) => void;
    advanceMemoryGrowth: (id: string) => void;
    addNewMemory: (memory: MemoryItem) => void;

    // Landmark zones
    landmarkZones: EnvironmentLandmarkZone[];
    nearbyZone: EnvironmentLandmarkZone | null;
    setNearbyZone: (zone: EnvironmentLandmarkZone | null) => void;

    // Memory interaction
    nearbyMemory: MemoryItem | null;
    setNearbyMemory: (memory: MemoryItem | null) => void;

    activeMemory: MemoryItem | null;
    openMemory: (memory: MemoryItem) => void;
    closeMemory: () => void;

    activeEchoId: string | null;
    setActiveEchoId: (id: string | null) => void;

    activeModal: ActiveModal;
    setActiveModal: (modal: ActiveModal) => void;

    discoveredIds: string[];
    markDiscovered: (id: string) => void;
}

export const useGameStore = create<GameState>((set) => ({
    phase: 'loading',
    setPhase: (phase) =>
        set({
            phase,
            // Automatically enter camera mode upon transitioning to exploring
            inputMode: phase === 'exploring' ? 'camera' : 'cursor',
        }),

    introStage: 1,
    setIntroStage: (introStage) => set({ introStage }),

    quality: 'high',
    toggleQuality: () =>
        set((state) => ({
            quality: state.quality === 'high' ? 'low' : 'high',
        })),

    inputMode: 'cursor',
    setInputMode: (inputMode) => set({ inputMode }),
    toggleInputMode: () =>
        set((state) => ({
            inputMode: state.inputMode === 'camera' ? 'cursor' : 'camera',
        })),

    characterModel: 'female',
    setCharacterModel: (characterModel) => set({ characterModel }),
    toggleCharacterModel: () =>
        set((state) => ({
            characterModel: state.characterModel === 'female' ? 'male' : 'female',
        })),

    isInitialTerrainReady: false,
    setInitialTerrainReady: (isInitialTerrainReady) =>
        set({ isInitialTerrainReady }),

    activeChunkCoord: [0, 0],
    setActiveChunkCoord: (coord) =>
        set((state) => {
            if (
                state.activeChunkCoord[0] === coord[0] &&
                state.activeChunkCoord[1] === coord[1]
            ) {
                return state;
            }
            return { activeChunkCoord: coord };
        }),

    persistentMemories: [],
    registerPersistentMemories: (memories) =>
        set((state) => {
            if (state.persistentMemories.length > 0) return state;
            return { persistentMemories: memories };
        }),

    advanceMemoryGrowth: (id) =>
        set((state) => ({
            persistentMemories: state.persistentMemories.map((m) => {
                if (m.id !== id) return m;
                const order: Array<MemoryItem['growthStage']> = [
                    'seed',
                    'plant',
                    'tree',
                    'landmark',
                ];
                const idx = order.indexOf(m.growthStage || 'seed');
                const nextStage = order[Math.min(idx + 1, order.length - 1)];
                return {
                    ...m,
                    growthStage: nextStage,
                    interactionState: 'awakened',
                    isDiscovered: true,
                };
            }),
        })),

    addNewMemory: (memory) =>
        set((state) => ({
            persistentMemories: [memory, ...state.persistentMemories],
            discoveredIds: [...state.discoveredIds, memory.id],
        })),

    landmarkZones: [
        {
            id: 'zone-sakura-kinship',
            name: 'Great Sakura Memory Tree',
            zoneType: 'memory_tree',
            position: [6.2, 0, 2.2],
            radius: 6.0,
            description: 'An ancient blossom tree preserving chapters of shared kinship.',
            associatedCollectionId: 'chapter-kinship',
            unlocked: true,
        },
        {
            id: 'zone-western-arch',
            name: 'Threshold of Hidden Pathways',
            zoneType: 'hidden_pathway',
            position: [-6.8, 0, -3.5],
            radius: 5.2,
            description: 'A moss-draped stone gate leading toward distant memory valleys.',
            associatedCollectionId: 'chapter-journeys',
            unlocked: true,
        },
        {
            id: 'zone-reflection-basin',
            name: 'Stillwater Reflection Glade',
            zoneType: 'reflection_pond',
            position: [-1.5, 0, -14.5],
            radius: 5.5,
            description: 'A quiet hollow where past seasons ripple across the surface.',
            unlocked: true,
        },
    ],
    nearbyZone: null,
    setNearbyZone: (zone) =>
        set((state) => {
            if (state.nearbyZone?.id === zone?.id) return state;
            return { nearbyZone: zone };
        }),

    nearbyMemory: null,
    setNearbyMemory: (memory) =>
        set((state) => {
            if (state.nearbyMemory?.id === memory?.id) return state;
            return { nearbyMemory: memory };
        }),

    activeMemory: null,
    openMemory: (memory) =>
        set((state) => ({
            activeMemory: memory,
            activeEchoId: memory.id,
            activeModal: 'memory',
            inputMode: 'cursor', // Opening memory viewer immediately enables cursor mode
            discoveredIds: state.discoveredIds.includes(memory.id)
                ? state.discoveredIds
                : [...state.discoveredIds, memory.id],
        })),
    closeMemory: () =>
        set({
            activeMemory: null,
            activeEchoId: null,
            activeModal: 'none',
            inputMode: 'camera', // Closing returns directly to camera mode
        }),

    activeEchoId: null,
    setActiveEchoId: (activeEchoId) => set({ activeEchoId }),

    activeModal: 'none',
    setActiveModal: (activeModal) =>
        set({
            activeModal,
            inputMode: activeModal === 'none' ? 'camera' : 'cursor',
        }),

    discoveredIds: [],
    markDiscovered: (id) =>
        set((state) => ({
            discoveredIds: state.discoveredIds.includes(id)
                ? state.discoveredIds
                : [...state.discoveredIds, id],
        })),
}));

// src/systems/Collision/collisionSystem.ts
import * as THREE from 'three';

export interface SolidCollider {
    id: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    z: number;
    radius: number;
    height: number;
    blocksCamera: boolean;
}

class SpatialCollisionManager {
    private chunkColliders = new Map<string, SolidCollider[]>();
    private staticColliders: SolidCollider[] = [];

    public setStaticColliders(colliders: SolidCollider[]) {
        this.staticColliders = colliders;
    }

    public registerChunk(chunkKey: string, colliders: SolidCollider[]) {
        this.chunkColliders.set(chunkKey, colliders);
    }

    public unregisterChunk(chunkKey: string) {
        this.chunkColliders.delete(chunkKey);
    }

    /**
     * Resolves horizontal cylinder pushback so the player cannot walk through
     * trees, boulders, archway pillars, or memory pedestals.
     */
    public resolvePlayerMovement(
        nextX: number,
        nextY: number,
        nextZ: number,
        playerRadius = 0.42
    ): [number, number] {
        let resolvedX = nextX;
        let resolvedZ = nextZ;

        const allLists = [this.staticColliders, ...this.chunkColliders.values()];

        for (const list of allLists) {
            for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
                const col = list[i];
                // Quick vertical overlap check
                if (nextY > col.y + col.height || nextY + 1.7 < col.y) continue;

                const dx = resolvedX - col.x;
                const dz = resolvedZ - col.z;
                const minDist = playerRadius + col.radius;
                const distSq = dx * dx + dz * dz;

                if (distSq < minDist * minDist && distSq > 0.00001) {
                    const dist = Math.sqrt(distSq);
                    const overlap = minDist - dist;
                    resolvedX += (dx / dist) * overlap;
                    resolvedZ += (dz / dist) * overlap;
                }
            }
        }

        return [resolvedX, resolvedZ];
    }

    /**
     * Multi-probe ray vs cylinder + bounding intersection for the Third-Person Camera.
     * Returns the maximum safe fraction [0.12 .. 1.0] along the pivot->camera segment.
     */
    public computeSafeCameraFraction(
        pivot: THREE.Vector3,
        desiredCam: THREE.Vector3,
        cameraMargin = 0.38
    ): number {
        const segDir = desiredCam.clone().sub(pivot);
        const segLen = segDir.length();
        if (segLen < 0.001) return 1.0;
        segDir.divideScalar(segLen);

        let minFraction = 1.0;
        const allLists = [this.staticColliders, ...this.chunkColliders.values()];

        for (const list of allLists) {
            for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
                const col = list[i];
                if (!col.blocksCamera) continue;

                // 2D ray-circle intersection in XZ plane with vertical height bounds check
                const ox = pivot.x - col.x;
                const oz = pivot.z - col.z;
                const dx = segDir.x;
                const dz = segDir.z;

                const a = dx * dx + dz * dz;
                if (a < 0.0001) continue;

                const r = col.radius + cameraMargin;
                const b = 2 * (ox * dx + oz * dz);
                const c = ox * ox + oz * oz - r * r;
                const disc = b * b - 4 * a * c;

                if (disc >= 0) {
                    const sqrtDisc = Math.sqrt(disc);
                    const t1 = (-b - sqrtDisc) / (2 * a);
                    if (t1 > 0.05 && t1 < segLen) {
                        const hitY = pivot.y + segDir.y * t1;
                        if (hitY >= col.y - 0.3 && hitY <= col.y + col.height + 0.3) {
                            const frac = Math.max(0.15, (t1 - 0.15) / segLen);
                            if (frac < minFraction) {
                                minFraction = frac;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        return minFraction;
    }
}

export const collisionManager = new SpatialCollisionManager();

export function exportObjectToGLB(object3D: THREE.Object3D, filename: string): void {
    const exporter = new GLTFExporter();
    exporter.parse(
        object3D,
        (result) => {
            if (result instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
                const blob = new Blob([result], { type: 'model/gltf-binary' });
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
                const link = document.createElement('a');
                link.href = url;
                link.download = filename.split('/').pop() || 'asset.glb';
                document.body.appendChild(link);
                link.click();
                document.body.removeChild(link);
                URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            }
        },
        (error) => {
            console.error('GLB Export Error:', error);
        },
        { binary: true }
    );
}

// src/scenes/GardenScene/GardenScene.tsx
import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import { Canvas, useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { GardenLighting } from '../Lighting/GardenLighting';
import { GardenEnvironment } from '../Environment/GardenEnvironment';
import { PlayerCharacter } from '../../components/Character/PlayerCharacter';
import { ThirdPersonCamera } from '../../components/Camera/ThirdPersonCamera';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import './GardenScene.css';

/**
 * Choreographs the 3D Camera during the Loading/Intro sequence before handing
 * control seamlessly to the ThirdPersonCamera upon entering the garden.
 */
const IntroCameraDirector: React.FC = () => {
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const introStage = useGameStore((state) => state.introStage);
    const lookAtTarget = useRef(new THREE.Vector3(-0.6, 5.2, -16));

    useFrame((state, delta) => {
        if (phase !== 'loading') return;

        const t = state.clock.getElapsedTime();
        const pointerX = state.pointer.x * 0.25;
        const pointerY = state.pointer.y * 0.14;

        const isSkyCanvasFrame = introStage <= 2;

        const desiredPos = new THREE.Vector3(
            pointerX,
            isSkyCanvasFrame ? 3.05 : 2.35 + Math.sin(t * 0.45) * 0.04,
            isSkyCanvasFrame ? 11.4 : 12.2
        );

        const desiredTarget = new THREE.Vector3(
            -0.4 + pointerX * 0.6,
            isSkyCanvasFrame ? 5.2 + pointerY : 2.5 + pointerY,
            -16
        );

        state.camera.position.lerp(desiredPos, delta * 1.65);
        lookAtTarget.current.lerp(desiredTarget, delta * 1.65);
        state.camera.lookAt(lookAtTarget.current);
    });

    return null;
};

export const GardenScene: React.FC = () => {
    const quality = useGameStore((state) => state.quality);

    return (
        <div className="garden-canvas-wrapper">
            <Canvas
                shadows
                dpr={quality === 'high' ? [1, 2] : [1, 1.25]}
                camera={{
                    position: [0, 3.05, 11.4],
                    fov: 42,
                    near: 0.1,
                    far: 260,
                }}
                gl={{
                    antialias: quality === 'high',
                    toneMapping: THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping,
                    toneMappingExposure: 1.06,
                    powerPreference: 'high-performance',
                }}
            >
                <GardenLighting />
                <GardenEnvironment />
                <PlayerCharacter />
                <IntroCameraDirector />
                <ThirdPersonCamera />
            </Canvas>
        </div>
    );
};

// src/scenes/Environment/ChunkedTerrain.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { THEME } from '../../styles/theme';
import {
    ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS,
    CHUNK_SEGMENTS,
    CHUNK_SIZE,
    createSculptedFacetedGeometry,
    getGardenTerrainHeight,
    getPathCenterX,
    hash2D,
    seededRandom,
} from './lowPolyUtils';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import {
    collisionManager,
    type SolidCollider,
} from '../../systems/Collision/collisionSystem';

const SHARED_GEOMETRIES = {
    trunk: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.18, 0.36, 2.2, 6),
    pineTier1: new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.35, 1.9, 7),
    pineTier2: new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.05, 1.65, 7),
    pineTier3: new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.72, 1.35, 7),
    canopyMain: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(1.35, 1, [1.15, 0.9, 1.1], 0.16, 12),
    canopySub: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(0.95, 1, [1.1, 0.85, 1.05], 0.18, 29),
    shrubGeo: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(0.55, 1, [1.25, 0.85, 1.15], 0.18, 63),
    boulder: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(0.85, 1, [1.3, 0.82, 1.15], 0.22, 41, true),
    mossCap: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(0.72, 1, [1.25, 0.35, 1.08], 0.15, 52),
    fallenLog: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22, 0.26, 2.6, 6),
    steppingStone: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.68, 0.78, 0.14, 7),
    grassBlade: new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.08, 0.42, 4),
    flowerHead: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.16, 0.08, 0.06, 5),
};

interface TreeData {
    id: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    z: number;
    scale: number;
    rotY: number;
    species: 'sakura' | 'pine' | 'oak' | 'shrub';
}

interface PropData {
    id: string;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    z: number;
    scale: [number, number, number];
    rot: [number, number, number];
    type: 'rock' | 'mossy_rock' | 'log' | 'stone';
    color: string;
}

interface TerrainChunkProps {
    cx: number;
    cz: number;
    onChunkReady?: (key: string) => void;
}

const TerrainChunk: React.FC<TerrainChunkProps> = React.memo(({ cx, cz, onChunkReady }) => {
    const chunkKey = `${cx}_${cz}`;
    const worldOriginX = cx * CHUNK_SIZE;
    const worldOriginZ = cz * CHUNK_SIZE;

    const grassMeshRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
    const flowerMeshRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
    const terrainMeshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);

    const terrainGeo = useMemo(() => {
        const plane = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(
            CHUNK_SIZE,
            CHUNK_SIZE,
            CHUNK_SEGMENTS,
            CHUNK_SEGMENTS
        );
        plane.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);

        const nonIndexed = plane.toNonIndexed();
        const pos = nonIndexed.attributes.position;
        const colors = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);

        const cShadow = new THREE.Color(THEME.colors.meadowShadow);
        const cBase = new THREE.Color(THEME.colors.meadowBase);
        const cSunlit = new THREE.Color(THEME.colors.meadowSunlit);
        const cPath = new THREE.Color('#7d7956');
        const temp = new THREE.Color();

        for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
            const lx = pos.getX(i);
            const lz = pos.getZ(i);
            const wx = lx + worldOriginX;
            const wz = lz + worldOriginZ;

            const wy = getGardenTerrainHeight(wx, wz);
            pos.setY(i, wy);

            const sunBlend = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
                0.5 + wy * 0.09 + Math.sin(wx * 0.08 - wz * 0.06) * 0.22,
                0,
                1
            );
            temp.copy(cBase).lerp(cSunlit, sunBlend * 0.78);

            if (wy < -0.25) {
                temp.lerp(cShadow, Math.min(Math.abs(wy + 0.25) * 0.4, 0.55));
            }

            const distToPath = Math.abs(wx - getPathCenterX(wz));
            if (distToPath < 2.2) {
                temp.lerp(cPath, (1 - distToPath / 2.2) * 0.42);
            }

            colors[i * 3] = temp.r;
            colors[i * 3 + 1] = temp.g;
            colors[i * 3 + 2] = temp.b;
        }

        nonIndexed.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
        nonIndexed.computeVertexNormals();

        return nonIndexed;
    }, [worldOriginX, worldOriginZ]);

    const { trees, propsList, grassTransforms, flowerTransforms, colliders } =
        useMemo(() => {
            const generatedTrees: TreeData[] = [];
            const generatedProps: PropData[] = [];
            const generatedColliders: SolidCollider[] = [];
            const grassMatrices: THREE.Matrix4[] = [];
            const flowerMatrices: THREE.Matrix4[] = [];

            const dummy = new THREE.Object3D();

            // Stepping Stones
            const zStart = worldOriginZ - CHUNK_SIZE / 2;
            const zEnd = worldOriginZ + CHUNK_SIZE / 2;
            const stepSpacing = 1.65;
            const firstStepIdx = Math.ceil(zStart / stepSpacing);
            const lastStepIdx = Math.floor(zEnd / stepSpacing);

            for (let sIdx = firstStepIdx; sIdx <= lastStepIdx; sIdx++) {
                const wz = sIdx * stepSpacing;
                const pathX = getPathCenterX(wz);
                const lateralJitter =
                    (sIdx % 2 === 0 ? 0.26 : -0.26) +
                    (seededRandom(sIdx * 19.3) - 0.5) * 0.18;
                const wx = pathX + lateralJitter;

                if (
                    wx >= worldOriginX - CHUNK_SIZE / 2 &&
                    wx < worldOriginX + CHUNK_SIZE / 2
                ) {
                    const wy = getGardenTerrainHeight(wx, wz);
                    generatedProps.push({
                        id: `stone_${chunkKey}_${sIdx}`,
                        x: wx - worldOriginX,
                        y: wy + 0.02,
                        z: wz - worldOriginZ,
                        scale: [
                            0.95 + seededRandom(sIdx * 7) * 0.35,
                            1,
                            0.8 + seededRandom(sIdx * 13) * 0.25,
                        ],
                        rot: [0, seededRandom(sIdx * 29) * Math.PI, 0],
                        type: 'stone',
                        color:
                            sIdx % 3 === 0 ? THEME.colors.stoneSunlit : THEME.colors.stoneBase,
                    });
                }
            }

            // Clustered Trees & Shrubs
            const treeSlots = 20;
            for (let i = 0; i < treeSlots; i++) {
                const seed = hash2D(cx * 37 + i, cz * 53 + i, 101);
                const lx = (hash2D(cx, cz, i * 11 + 1) - 0.5) * (CHUNK_SIZE - 4);
                const lz = (hash2D(cx, cz, i * 11 + 2) - 0.5) * (CHUNK_SIZE - 4);
                const wx = worldOriginX + lx;
                const wz = worldOriginZ + lz;

                if (Math.abs(wx - getPathCenterX(wz)) < 3.8) continue;
                if (Math.hypot(wx, wz - 7.5) < 4.8) continue;
                // Keep Sanctuary landmarks clear in chunk (0,0)
                if (cx === 0 && cz === 0 && Math.hypot(wx, wz) < 16.5) continue;

                const groveDensity =
                    Math.sin(wx * 0.065) * Math.cos(wz * 0.065) * 0.5 + 0.5;
                if (seed > groveDensity + 0.32) continue;

                const wy = getGardenTerrainHeight(wx, wz);
                const scale = 0.78 + seededRandom(seed * 97) * 0.75;
                const speciesRoll = seededRandom(seed * 43);
                const species: TreeData['species'] =
                    speciesRoll < 0.28
                        ? 'sakura'
                        : speciesRoll < 0.56
                            ? 'oak'
                            : speciesRoll < 0.82
                                ? 'pine'
                                : 'shrub';

                generatedTrees.push({
                    id: `tree_${chunkKey}_${i}`,
                    x: lx,
                    y: wy - 0.04,
                    z: lz,
                    scale,
                    rotY: seededRandom(seed * 61) * Math.PI * 2,
                    species,
                });

                if (species !== 'shrub') {
                    generatedColliders.push({
                        id: `col_tree_${chunkKey}_${i}`,
                        x: wx,
                        y: wy,
                        z: wz,
                        radius: 0.42 * scale,
                        height: 4.2 * scale,
                        blocksCamera: true,
                    });
                }
            }

            // Boulders, Mossy Rocks & Fallen Logs
            const rockCount = 12;
            for (let r = 0; r < rockCount; r++) {
                const rSeed = hash2D(cx * 19 + r, cz * 29 + r, 303);
                const lx = (hash2D(cx, cz, r * 17 + 70) - 0.5) * (CHUNK_SIZE - 3);
                const lz = (hash2D(cx, cz, r * 17 + 71) - 0.5) * (CHUNK_SIZE - 3);
                const wx = worldOriginX + lx;
                const wz = worldOriginZ + lz;

                if (Math.abs(wx - getPathCenterX(wz)) < 2.8) continue;
                if (Math.hypot(wx, wz - 7.5) < 3.8) continue;
                if (cx === 0 && cz === 0 && Math.hypot(wx, wz) < 14.5) continue;

                const wy = getGardenTerrainHeight(wx, wz);
                const isFallenLog = r % 5 === 0 && rSeed > 0.35;

                if (isFallenLog) {
                    const logScale = 0.75 + rSeed * 0.45;
                    generatedProps.push({
                        id: `log_${chunkKey}_${r}`,
                        x: lx,
                        y: wy + 0.08,
                        z: lz,
                        scale: [logScale, logScale, logScale],
                        rot: [
                            Math.PI / 2 + (rSeed - 0.5) * 0.12,
                            rSeed * Math.PI,
                            (rSeed - 0.5) * 0.15,
                        ],
                        type: 'log',
                        color: THEME.colors.barkBase,
                    });
                } else {
                    const sx = 0.6 + rSeed * 0.9;
                    const sy = 0.5 + seededRandom(rSeed * 13) * 0.65;
                    const sz = 0.6 + seededRandom(rSeed * 29) * 0.85;
                    generatedProps.push({
                        id: `rock_${chunkKey}_${r}`,
                        x: lx,
                        y: wy - 0.12,
                        z: lz,
                        scale: [sx, sy, sz],
                        rot: [0, rSeed * Math.PI * 2, 0],
                        type: r % 3 === 0 ? 'mossy_rock' : 'rock',
                        color:
                            r % 2 === 0 ? THEME.colors.stoneBase : THEME.colors.stoneSunlit,
                    });

                    if (sx > 0.82) {
                        generatedColliders.push({
                            id: `col_rock_${chunkKey}_${r}`,
                            x: wx,
                            y: wy,
                            z: wz,
                            radius: 0.68 * sx,
                            height: 1.1 * sy,
                            blocksCamera: sx > 1.1,
                        });
                    }
                }
            }

            // Instanced Grass & Wildflowers
            const grassCount = 145;
            for (let g = 0; g < grassCount; g++) {
                const lx = (hash2D(cx, cz, g * 3 + 500) - 0.5) * CHUNK_SIZE;
                const lz = (hash2D(cx, cz, g * 3 + 501) - 0.5) * CHUNK_SIZE;
                const wx = worldOriginX + lx;
                const wz = worldOriginZ + lz;

                if (Math.abs(wx - getPathCenterX(wz)) < 1.35) continue;

                const wy = getGardenTerrainHeight(wx, wz);
                const s = 0.65 + hash2D(cx, cz, g * 3 + 502) * 0.85;

                dummy.position.set(lx, wy + 0.12 * s, lz);
                dummy.rotation.set(
                    (seededRandom(g * 7) - 0.5) * 0.25,
                    seededRandom(g * 13) * Math.PI * 2,
                    (seededRandom(g * 19) - 0.5) * 0.25
                );
                dummy.scale.set(s, s, s);
                dummy.updateMatrix();
                grassMatrices.push(dummy.matrix.clone());

                if (g % 5 === 0) {
                    dummy.position.set(lx, wy + 0.3 * s, lz);
                    dummy.rotation.set(0.2, seededRandom(g * 31) * Math.PI * 2, 0);
                    dummy.scale.set(s * 0.9, s * 0.9, s * 0.9);
                    dummy.updateMatrix();
                    flowerMatrices.push(dummy.matrix.clone());
                }
            }

            return {
                trees: generatedTrees,
                propsList: generatedProps,
                grassTransforms: grassMatrices,
                flowerTransforms: flowerMatrices,
                colliders: generatedColliders,
            };
        }, [cx, cz, chunkKey, worldOriginX, worldOriginZ]);

    useLayoutEffect(() => {
        if (grassMeshRef.current) {
            grassTransforms.forEach((mat, idx) => {
                grassMeshRef.current!.setMatrixAt(idx, mat);
            });
            grassMeshRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
        }
        if (flowerMeshRef.current) {
            flowerTransforms.forEach((mat, idx) => {
                flowerMeshRef.current!.setMatrixAt(idx, mat);
            });
            flowerMeshRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
        }
        onChunkReady?.(chunkKey);
    }, [grassTransforms, flowerTransforms, onChunkReady, chunkKey]);

    useEffect(() => {
        collisionManager.registerChunk(chunkKey, colliders);
        return () => {
            collisionManager.unregisterChunk(chunkKey);
            terrainGeo.dispose();
        };
    }, [chunkKey, colliders, terrainGeo]);

    const customTerrainMaterial = useMemo(() => {
        const mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
            vertexColors: true,
            roughness: 0.92,
            metalness: 0.02,
            flatShading: true,
        });

        mat.onBeforeCompile = (shader) => {
            shader.uniforms.uMemoryCenter = { value: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0) };
            shader.uniforms.uRadianceRadius = { value: 7.5 };
            shader.uniforms.uRadianceStrength = { value: 0.0 };

            mat.userData.shader = shader;

            shader.fragmentShader =
                `
        uniform vec3 uMemoryCenter;
        uniform float uRadianceRadius;
        uniform float uRadianceStrength;
        varying vec3 vWorldPos;
      ` + shader.fragmentShader;

            shader.fragmentShader = shader.fragmentShader.replace(
                '#include <color_fragment>',
                `
        #include <color_fragment>
        float memDist = distance(vWorldPos, uMemoryCenter);
        float radianceFactor = clamp(1.0 - (memDist / uRadianceRadius), 0.0, 1.0);
        radianceFactor = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, radianceFactor) * uRadianceStrength;
        
        vec3 goldenGlow = vec3(1.15, 0.98, 0.65);
        diffuseColor.rgb = mix(diffuseColor.rgb, diffuseColor.rgb * goldenGlow + vec3(0.12, 0.08, 0.02), radianceFactor * 0.65);
        `
            );

            shader.vertexShader =
                `
        varying vec3 vWorldPos;
      ` + shader.vertexShader;

            shader.vertexShader = shader.vertexShader.replace(
                '#include <worldpos_vertex>',
                `
        #include <worldpos_vertex>
        vWorldPos = (modelMatrix * vec4(transformed, 1.0)).xyz;
        `
            );
        };

        return mat;
    }, []);

    useFrame(() => {
        if (terrainMeshRef.current) {
            const mat = terrainMeshRef.current.material as THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;
            const shader = mat.userData.shader;
            if (shader) {
                const [mx, my, mz] = playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryPosition;
                shader.uniforms.uMemoryCenter.value.set(mx, my, mz);
                shader.uniforms.uRadianceStrength.value =
                    playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryRadiance;
            }
        }
    });

    return (
        <group position={[worldOriginX, 0, worldOriginZ]}>
            <mesh
                ref={terrainMeshRef}
                geometry={terrainGeo}
                material={customTerrainMaterial}
                receiveShadow
            />

            {trees.map((t) => (
                <group
                    key={t.id}
                    position={[t.x, t.y, t.z]}
                    rotation={[0, t.rotY, 0]}
                    scale={t.scale}
                >
                    {t.species === 'shrub' ? (
                        <mesh
                            geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.shrubGeo}
                            position={[0, 0.38, 0]}
                            castShadow
                            receiveShadow
                        >
                            <meshStandardMaterial
                                color={THEME.colors.meadowBase}
                                roughness={0.85}
                                flatShading
                            />
                        </mesh>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <mesh
                                geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.trunk}
                                position={[0, 1.0, 0]}
                                castShadow
                                receiveShadow
                            >
                                <meshStandardMaterial
                                    color={THEME.colors.barkBase}
                                    roughness={0.9}
                                    flatShading
                                />
                            </mesh>

                            {t.species === 'pine' ? (
                                <group position={[0, 1.85, 0]}>
                                    <mesh
                                        geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.pineTier1}
                                        position={[0, 0, 0]}
                                        castShadow
                                        receiveShadow
                                    >
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={THEME.colors.canopyDark}
                                            roughness={0.85}
                                            flatShading
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh
                                        geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.pineTier2}
                                        position={[0, 1.0, 0]}
                                        castShadow
                                        receiveShadow
                                    >
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={THEME.colors.meadowBase}
                                            roughness={0.85}
                                            flatShading
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh
                                        geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.pineTier3}
                                        position={[0, 1.85, 0]}
                                        castShadow
                                        receiveShadow
                                    >
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={THEME.colors.canopyLight}
                                            roughness={0.85}
                                            flatShading
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                </group>
                            ) : (
                                <group position={[0, 2.55, 0]}>
                                    <mesh
                                        geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.canopyMain}
                                        position={[0, 0.15, 0]}
                                        castShadow
                                        receiveShadow
                                    >
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={
                                                t.species === 'sakura'
                                                    ? THEME.colors.sakuraMid
                                                    : THEME.colors.meadowBase
                                            }
                                            roughness={0.8}
                                            flatShading
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh
                                        geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.canopySub}
                                        position={[-0.85, -0.25, 0.45]}
                                        castShadow
                                        receiveShadow
                                    >
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={
                                                t.species === 'sakura'
                                                    ? THEME.colors.sakuraLight
                                                    : THEME.colors.meadowSunlit
                                            }
                                            roughness={0.8}
                                            flatShading
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh
                                        geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.canopySub}
                                        position={[0.85, -0.15, -0.35]}
                                        castShadow
                                        receiveShadow
                                    >
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={
                                                t.species === 'sakura'
                                                    ? THEME.colors.sakuraDeep
                                                    : THEME.colors.canopyOlive
                                            }
                                            roughness={0.8}
                                            flatShading
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                </group>
                            )}
                        </>
                    )}
                </group>
            ))}

            {propsList.map((p) => (
                <group
                    key={p.id}
                    position={[p.x, p.y, p.z]}
                    rotation={p.rot}
                    scale={p.scale}
                >
                    <mesh
                        geometry={
                            p.type === 'rock' || p.type === 'mossy_rock'
                                ? SHARED_GEOMETRIES.boulder
                                : p.type === 'log'
                                    ? SHARED_GEOMETRIES.fallenLog
                                    : SHARED_GEOMETRIES.steppingStone
                        }
                        castShadow={p.type !== 'stone'}
                        receiveShadow
                    >
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={p.color}
                            roughness={0.88}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                    {p.type === 'mossy_rock' && (
                        <mesh
                            geometry={SHARED_GEOMETRIES.mossCap}
                            position={[0, 0.55, 0]}
                            castShadow
                        >
                            <meshStandardMaterial
                                color={THEME.colors.meadowBase}
                                roughness={0.85}
                                flatShading
                            />
                        </mesh>
                    )}
                </group>
            ))}

            {grassTransforms.length > 0 && (
                <instancedMesh
                    ref={grassMeshRef}
                    args={[
                        SHARED_GEOMETRIES.grassBlade,
                        undefined,
                        grassTransforms.length,
                    ]}
                    frustumCulled={false}
                >
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={THEME.colors.grassTuftLight}
                        roughness={0.86}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </instancedMesh>
            )}

            {flowerTransforms.length > 0 && (
                <instancedMesh
                    ref={flowerMeshRef}
                    args={[
                        SHARED_GEOMETRIES.flowerHead,
                        undefined,
                        flowerTransforms.length,
                    ]}
                    frustumCulled={false}
                >
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={THEME.colors.flowerPetal}
                        roughness={0.7}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </instancedMesh>
            )}
        </group>
    );
});

export const ChunkedTerrain: React.FC = () => {
    const activeChunkCoord = useGameStore((state) => state.activeChunkCoord);
    const setActiveChunkCoord = useGameStore((state) => state.setActiveChunkCoord);
    const setInitialTerrainReady = useGameStore(
        (state) => state.setInitialTerrainReady
    );

    const readyChunks = useRef(new Set<string>());

    const handleChunkReady = (key: string) => {
        readyChunks.current.add(key);
        if (readyChunks.current.has('0_0')) {
            setInitialTerrainReady(true);
        }
    };

    useFrame(() => {
        const px = playerTelemetry.position[0];
        const pz = playerTelemetry.position[2];
        const cx = Math.round(px / CHUNK_SIZE);
        const cz = Math.round(pz / CHUNK_SIZE);
        if (cx !== activeChunkCoord[0] || cz !== activeChunkCoord[1]) {
            setActiveChunkCoord([cx, cz]);
        }
    });

    const visibleChunks = useMemo(() => {
        const coords: Array<[number, number]> = [];
        for (let dx = -ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS; dx <= ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS; dx++) {
            for (let dz = -ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS; dz <= ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS; dz++) {
                coords.push([activeChunkCoord[0] + dx, activeChunkCoord[1] + dz]);
            }
        }
        return coords;
    }, [activeChunkCoord]);

    return (
        <group>
            {visibleChunks.map(([cx, cz]) => (
                <TerrainChunk
                    key={`${cx}_${cz}`}
                    cx={cx}
                    cz={cz}
                    onChunkReady={handleChunkReady}
                />
            ))}
        </group>
    );
};

// src/scenes/Environment/GardenEnvironment.tsx

    createSculptedFacetedGeometry,
    getGardenTerrainHeight,
    seededRandom,
} from './lowPolyUtils';

const StoneArchwayRuin: React.FC<{
    position: [number, number, number];
    rotationY?: number;
    scale?: number;
}> = ({ position, rotationY = 0.34, scale = 1.08 }) => {
    const ivyClusters = useMemo(() => {
        return [
            { pos: [-1.7, 4.9, 0.35] as [number, number, number], s: [0.9, 1.3, 0.6] as [number, number, number], seed: 11 },
            { pos: [-1.85, 3.6, 0.42] as [number, number, number], s: [0.65, 1.5, 0.5] as [number, number, number], seed: 22 },
            { pos: [-0.6, 5.7, 0.35] as [number, number, number], s: [1.3, 0.85, 0.65] as [number, number, number], seed: 33 },
            { pos: [0.65, 5.5, 0.35] as [number, number, number], s: [1.1, 0.9, 0.6] as [number, number, number], seed: 44 },
            { pos: [1.65, 4.4, 0.38] as [number, number, number], s: [0.75, 1.4, 0.55] as [number, number, number], seed: 55 },
        ].map((item) => ({
            ...item,
            geo: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(0.65, 1, item.s, 0.22, item.seed),
        }));
    }, []);

    const archBlocks = useMemo(() => {
        const blocks: Array<{
            pos: [number, number, number];
            rotZ: number;
            size: [number, number, number];
        }> = [];
        const count = 9;
        const radius = 1.75;
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const angle = Math.PI - (i / (count - 1)) * Math.PI;
            blocks.push({
                pos: [Math.cos(angle) * radius, 3.85 + Math.sin(angle) * radius, 0],
                rotZ: angle - Math.PI / 2,
                size: [0.72, 0.88, 1.08],
            });
        }
        return blocks;
    }, []);

    return (
        <group position={position} rotation={[0, rotationY, 0]} scale={scale}>
            <mesh position={[-1.75, 0.3, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                <boxGeometry args={[1.65, 0.6, 1.65]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.stoneShadow} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
            </mesh>
            <mesh position={[1.75, 0.3, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                <boxGeometry args={[1.65, 0.6, 1.65]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.stoneShadow} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
            </mesh>

            {[0.95, 1.85, 2.75, 3.6].map((py, idx) => (
                <React.Fragment key={idx}>
                    <mesh position={[-1.75, py, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                        <boxGeometry args={[1.16, 0.86, 1.15]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={idx % 2 === 0 ? THEME.colors.stoneBase : THEME.colors.stoneSunlit}
                            roughness={0.88}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                    <mesh position={[1.75, py, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                        <boxGeometry args={[1.16, 0.86, 1.15]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={idx % 2 === 1 ? THEME.colors.stoneBase : THEME.colors.stoneSunlit}
                            roughness={0.88}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                </React.Fragment>
            ))}

            {archBlocks.map((b, i) => (
                <mesh
                    key={`arch-${i}`}
                    position={b.pos}
                    rotation={[0, 0, b.rotZ]}
                    castShadow
                    receiveShadow
                >
                    <boxGeometry args={b.size} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={i % 2 === 0 ? THEME.colors.stoneSunlit : THEME.colors.stoneBase}
                        roughness={0.86}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>
            ))}

            {ivyClusters.map((ivy, idx) => (
                <mesh key={`ivy-${idx}`} geometry={ivy.geo} position={ivy.pos} castShadow>
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={idx % 2 === 0 ? THEME.colors.ivyGreen : THEME.colors.ivyLight}
                        roughness={0.85}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>
            ))}
        </group>
    );
};

const GiantSakuraTree: React.FC<{ position: [number, number, number] }> = ({
    position,
}) => {
    const banner1Ref = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const banner2Ref = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const canopyGroupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);

    const blossomClusters = useMemo(() => {
        const specs: Array<{
            pos: [number, number, number];
            scale: [number, number, number];
            radius: number;
            color: string;
            seed: number;
        }> = [
                { pos: [-3.8, 7.6, 1.4], scale: [1.65, 0.92, 1.35], radius: 1.85, color: THEME.colors.sakuraLight, seed: 101 },
                { pos: [-2.1, 8.4, 0.8], scale: [1.85, 1.05, 1.5], radius: 2.1, color: THEME.colors.sakuraMid, seed: 102 },
                { pos: [-0.2, 8.9, 0.2], scale: [2.1, 1.15, 1.7], radius: 2.3, color: THEME.colors.sakuraDeep, seed: 103 },
                { pos: [1.8, 8.2, -0.4], scale: [1.9, 1.1, 1.6], radius: 2.2, color: THEME.colors.sakuraMid, seed: 104 },
                { pos: [-4.9, 6.9, 1.9], scale: [1.35, 0.82, 1.15], radius: 1.55, color: THEME.colors.sakuraRim, seed: 105 },
                { pos: [-2.6, 6.4, 1.8], scale: [1.45, 0.85, 1.2], radius: 1.6, color: THEME.colors.sakuraLight, seed: 106 },
                { pos: [-1.1, 7.1, 2.2], scale: [1.5, 0.9, 1.3], radius: 1.75, color: THEME.colors.sakuraMid, seed: 107 },
                { pos: [0.9, 6.8, 1.6], scale: [1.65, 0.95, 1.4], radius: 1.85, color: THEME.colors.sakuraDeep, seed: 108 },
                { pos: [-3.2, 9.3, -0.3], scale: [1.6, 0.95, 1.4], radius: 1.9, color: THEME.colors.sakuraRim, seed: 109 },
            ];

        return specs.map((s) => ({
            ...s,
            geo: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(s.radius, 1, s.scale, 0.18, s.seed),
        }));
    }, []);

    useFrame(({ clock }) => {
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        if (banner1Ref.current) {
            banner1Ref.current.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 1.3) * 0.045 + 0.03;
            banner1Ref.current.rotation.x = Math.cos(t * 0.9) * 0.03;
        }
        if (banner2Ref.current) {
            banner2Ref.current.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 1.3 + 1.1) * 0.05 + 0.04;
            banner2Ref.current.rotation.x = Math.cos(t * 0.9 + 0.8) * 0.035;
        }
        if (canopyGroupRef.current) {
            canopyGroupRef.current.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 0.6) * 0.008;
        }
    });

    return (
        <group position={position}>
            {[
                { pos: [-1.1, 0.45, 0.9], rot: [0.3, 0.4, 0.48], size: [0.55, 0.95, 2.4] },
                { pos: [-0.6, 0.35, 1.35], rot: [0.45, 0.1, 0.25], size: [0.45, 0.8, 2.1] },
                { pos: [0.4, 0.4, 1.1], rot: [0.35, -0.2, -0.2], size: [0.5, 0.85, 2.0] },
                { pos: [-1.3, 0.4, -0.4], rot: [-0.2, 0.5, 0.52], size: [0.5, 0.9, 2.2] },
            ].map((r, i) => (
                <mesh
                    key={`root-${i}`}
                    position={r.pos as [number, number, number]}
                    rotation={r.rot as [number, number, number]}
                    castShadow
                    receiveShadow
                >
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[r.size[0], r.size[1], r.size[2], 6]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={i % 2 === 0 ? THEME.colors.barkHighlight : THEME.colors.barkBase}
                        roughness={0.9}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>
            ))}

            <mesh position={[0, 2.1, 0]} rotation={[0.04, 0.2, 0.12]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                <cylinderGeometry args={[1.05, 1.65, 4.4, 7]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.barkBase} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
            </mesh>
            <mesh position={[-0.38, 4.6, 0.15]} rotation={[0.06, -0.3, 0.22]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                <cylinderGeometry args={[0.78, 1.12, 3.2, 7]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.barkHighlight} roughness={0.88} flatShading />
            </mesh>

            <mesh position={[-1.95, 5.95, 0.75]} rotation={[0.18, 0.15, 0.88]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                <cylinderGeometry args={[0.32, 0.68, 4.2, 6]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.barkBase} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
            </mesh>
            <mesh position={[-3.7, 7.1, 1.3]} rotation={[0.15, 0.1, 1.12]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                <cylinderGeometry args={[0.18, 0.34, 2.8, 6]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.barkShadow} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
            </mesh>

            <group ref={banner1Ref} position={[-2.25, 5.65, 0.92]}>
                <mesh position={[0, -0.95, 0]} castShadow>
                    <boxGeometry args={[0.68, 1.45, 0.03]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={THEME.colors.bannerFabric} roughness={0.85} flatShading />
                </mesh>
            </group>

            <group ref={banner2Ref} position={[-1.25, 5.2, 0.65]}>
                <mesh position={[0, -0.82, 0]} castShadow>
                    <boxGeometry args={[0.56, 1.22, 0.03]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color="#cfa87e" roughness={0.85} flatShading />
                </mesh>
            </group>

            <group ref={canopyGroupRef}>
                {blossomClusters.map((c, idx) => (
                    <mesh
                        key={`blossom-${idx}`}
                        geometry={c.geo}
                        position={c.pos}
                        castShadow
                        receiveShadow
                    >
                        <meshStandardMaterial color={c.color} roughness={0.78} flatShading />
                    </mesh>
                ))}
            </group>
        </group>
    );
};

const WorldSpaceBlossomPetals: React.FC = () => {
    const instancedRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
    const dummy = useMemo(() => new THREE.Object3D(), []);
    const petalGeo = useMemo(() => new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1, 0), []);

    const particles = useMemo(
        () =>
            Array.from({ length: 24 }).map((_, i) => ({
                wx: (seededRandom(i * 13 + 1) - 0.5) * 44,
                wy: 1.5 + seededRandom(i * 29 + 2) * 8.5,
                wz: (seededRandom(i * 47 + 3) - 0.5) * 44,
                vx: -0.55 - seededRandom(i * 61) * 0.35,
                vy: -0.62 - seededRandom(i * 73) * 0.28,
                vz: 0.15 + (seededRandom(i * 89) - 0.5) * 0.35,
                phase: seededRandom(i * 97) * Math.PI * 2,
                rotSpeedX: 0.9 + seededRandom(i * 103) * 1.1,
                rotSpeedY: 0.7 + seededRandom(i * 109) * 1.0,
            })),
        []
    );

    useFrame(({ clock }, rawDelta) => {
        if (!instancedRef.current) return;
        const delta = Math.min(rawDelta, 0.05);
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        const [px, , pz] = playerTelemetry.position;
        const maxRadius = 28;

        for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
            const p = particles[i];

            p.wx += (p.vx + Math.sin(t * 1.4 + p.phase) * 0.28) * delta;
            p.wy += (p.vy + Math.cos(t * 2.1 + p.phase) * 0.12) * delta;
            p.wz += (p.vz + Math.cos(t * 1.2 + p.phase) * 0.24) * delta;

            const groundY = getGardenTerrainHeight(p.wx, p.wz);

            if (p.wy < groundY + 0.15) {
                p.wy = groundY + 7.5 + seededRandom(i + t) * 3.5;
                p.wx = px + (seededRandom(i * 19 + t) - 0.5) * 38;
                p.wz = pz + (seededRandom(i * 31 + t) - 0.5) * 38;
            }

            const dx = p.wx - px;
            const dz = p.wz - pz;
            if (dx > maxRadius) p.wx -= maxRadius * 2;
            else if (dx < -maxRadius) p.wx += maxRadius * 2;

            if (dz > maxRadius) p.wz -= maxRadius * 2;
            else if (dz < -maxRadius) p.wz += maxRadius * 2;

            dummy.position.set(p.wx, p.wy, p.wz);
            dummy.rotation.set(
                t * p.rotSpeedX + p.phase,
                t * p.rotSpeedY + p.phase,
                Math.sin(t + p.phase) * 0.5
            );
            dummy.scale.set(0.085, 0.022, 0.052);
            dummy.updateMatrix();
            instancedRef.current.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
        }

        instancedRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
    });

    return (
        <instancedMesh
            ref={instancedRef}
            args={[petalGeo, undefined, particles.length]}
            frustumCulled={false}
        >
            <meshBasicMaterial color="#f5b8b0" transparent opacity={0.86} />
        </instancedMesh>
    );
};

                </group>
            </group>

            {[

};

// src/scenes/Environment/MemoryRadiance.tsx
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import { THEME } from '../../styles/theme';

export const MemoryRadiance: React.FC = () => {
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const persistentMemories = useGameStore((state) => state.persistentMemories);

    const motesMeshRef = useRef<THREE.InstancedMesh>(null);
    const dummy = useMemo(() => new THREE.Object3D(), []);

    // Ray cones and volumetric motes around active memories
    const motes = useMemo(
        () =>
            Array.from({ length: 48 }).map((_, i) => ({
                offsetAngle: (i / 48) * Math.PI * 2,
                dist: 0.6 + (i % 6) * 0.75,
                baseY: 0.4 + (i % 8) * 0.32,
                speed: 0.8 + (i % 4) * 0.4,
                scale: 0.04 + (i % 3) * 0.025,
            })),
        []
    );

    useFrame(({ clock }, delta) => {
        if (phase !== 'exploring') return;
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        const [px, , pz] = playerTelemetry.position;

        // 1. Calculate nearest memory and smooth radial falloff
        let minDist = 999;
        let nearestPos: [number, number, number] = [0, 0, 0];

        persistentMemories.forEach((mem) => {
            const d = Math.hypot(px - mem.position[0], pz - mem.position[2]);
            if (d < minDist) {
                minDist = d;
                nearestPos = [mem.position[0], mem.position[1], mem.position[2]];
            }
        });

        playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryDistance = minDist;
        playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryPosition = nearestPos;

        // Smooth radiance falloff (0 outside 8m, ramp to 1 at <2.5m)
        const targetRadiance =
            minDist < 8.0 ? Math.pow(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1.0 - minDist / 8.0, 0, 1), 1.25) : 0;

        playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryRadiance = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
            playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryRadiance,
            targetRadiance,
            delta * 4.5
        );

        const rad = playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryRadiance;

        // 2. Animate Golden Motes rising in the illuminated memory zone
        if (motesMeshRef.current && rad > 0.05) {
            motes.forEach((m, idx) => {
                const angle = m.offsetAngle + t * 0.15 * m.speed;
                const r = m.dist * (0.8 + Math.sin(t * 1.5 + idx) * 0.2);
                const wx = nearestPos[0] + Math.cos(angle) * r;
                const wz = nearestPos[2] + Math.sin(angle) * r;
                const wy = nearestPos[1] + ((m.baseY + t * 0.45 * m.speed) % 3.6);

                dummy.position.set(wx, wy, wz);
                const s = m.scale * (0.4 + rad * 0.6) * (1.0 - wy / 4.0);
                dummy.scale.set(s, s, s);
                dummy.updateMatrix();
                motesMeshRef.current!.setMatrixAt(idx, dummy.matrix);
            });
            motesMeshRef.current.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
        }
    });

    if (phase !== 'exploring') return null;

    return (
        <group>
            {/* Volumetric Soft Rays over Active Memories */}
            {persistentMemories.map((mem) => (
                <group key={`radiance-rays-${mem.id}`} position={mem.position}>
                    {/* Gentle Downward Angled Volumetric Light Cone */}
                    <mesh position={[0, 2.2, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI]}>
                        <coneGeometry args={[2.8, 4.4, 16, 1, true]} />
                        <meshBasicMaterial
                            color={THEME.colors.memoryGold}
                            transparent
                            opacity={0.14}
                            side={THREE.DoubleSide}
                            blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
                            depthWrite={false}
                        />
                    </mesh>
                </group>
            ))}

            {/* Radiant Floating Motes */}
            <instancedMesh
                ref={motesMeshRef}
                args={[new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1, 0), undefined, motes.length]}
                frustumCulled={false}
            >
                <meshBasicMaterial
                    color={THEME.colors.memoryCore}
                    transparent
                    opacity={0.85}
                    blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
                />
            </instancedMesh>
        </group>
    );
};

FILE #25 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils.ts
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\scenes\Environment\lowPolyUtils.ts

DESCRIPTION   : Procedural geometry utilities, pseudo-random seed generator, and Simplex noise algorithms.

// src/scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils.ts
import * as THREE from 'three';

export const CHUNK_SIZE = 40;
export const CHUNK_SEGMENTS = 28;
export const ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS = 1; // 3x3 chunk grid = 120m x 120m active around player

export function seededRandom(seed: number): number {
    const x = Math.sin(seed * 127.1 + 311.7) * 43758.5453123;
    return x - Math.floor(x);
}

export function hash2D(ix: number, iz: number, salt = 0): number {
    const dot = ix * 127.1 + iz * 311.7 + salt * 74.7;
    const s = Math.sin(dot) * 43758.5453123;
    return s - Math.floor(s);
}

export function smoothstep(x: number, min: number, max: number): number {
    const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (x - min) / (max - min)));
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}

/**
 * Continuous 2D Value Noise for seamless cross-chunk terrain generation
 */
export function valueNoise2D(x: number, z: number, salt = 0): number {
    const ix = Math.floor(x);
    const iz = Math.floor(z);
    const fx = x - ix;
    const fz = z - iz;

    const u = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
    const v = fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz);

    const a = hash2D(ix, iz, salt);
    const b = hash2D(ix + 1, iz, salt);
    const c = hash2D(ix, iz + 1, salt);
    const d = hash2D(ix + 1, iz + 1, salt);

    return a + (b - a) * u + (c - a) * v + (a - b - c + d) * u * v;
}

/**
 * Multi-octave Fractal Brownian Motion (fBM)
 */
export function fbm2D(x: number, z: number, octaves = 3): number {
    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 0.5;
    let frequency = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        value += amplitude * (valueNoise2D(x * frequency, z * frequency, i * 17) * 2 - 1);
        frequency *= 2.04;
        amplitude *= 0.5;
    }
    return value;
}

/**
 * Returns the exact X-center of the meandering garden trail for any Z coordinate
 */
export function getPathCenterX(z: number): number {
    return Math.sin(z * 0.075) * 2.4 + Math.cos(z * 0.032) * 1.3 - 0.5;
}

/**
 * Seamless World-Space Terrain Height Function.
 * Because this depends purely on global (x, z), adjacent chunk edges match with
 * 0.00000m error while blending the curated Sanctuary Basin with infinite rolling hills.
 */
export function getGardenTerrainHeight(x: number, z: number): number {
    const pathX = getPathCenterX(z);
    const distFromPath = Math.abs(x - pathX);

    // Smooth flat-bottomed walking valley along the meandering trail
    const pathFlatten = smoothstep(distFromPath, 1.4, 7.5);

    // Sanctuary Basin near origin so the hero Sakura Tree & Stone Archway sit naturally
    const distFromOrigin = Math.sqrt(x * x + z * z);
    const sanctuaryBlend = smoothstep(distFromOrigin, 12, 38);

    // Curated hero knolls near origin (Left archway ridge & Right sakura knoll)
    const leftArchHill =
        x < -3.5 ? smoothstep(Math.abs(x + 3.5), 0, 18) * 1.95 * (1 - sanctuaryBlend * 0.5) : 0;
    const rightSakuraKnoll =
        x > 3.5 ? smoothstep(x - 3.5, 0, 16) * 2.15 * (1 - sanctuaryBlend * 0.5) : 0;

    // Continuous procedural rolling hills & gentle valleys across the infinite world
    const macroHills = fbm2D(x * 0.018, z * 0.018, 3) * 4.6;
    const mesoSlopes = fbm2D(x * 0.055 + 11.3, z * 0.055 - 7.9, 2) * 1.15;
    const microUndulation = Math.sin(x * 0.19 + z * 0.15) * 0.11;

    const rawElevation =
        (macroHills * (0.35 + 0.65 * sanctuaryBlend) +
            mesoSlopes * (0.4 + 0.6 * sanctuaryBlend) +
            leftArchHill +
            rightSakuraKnoll +
            microUndulation) *
        pathFlatten;

    // Slight path depression so flagstones sit nestled in the meadow
    const pathChannel = (1 - pathFlatten) * -0.18;

    return rawElevation + pathChannel - 0.35;
}

/**
 * Computes terrain surface normal via finite differences for slope handling
 */
export function getGardenTerrainNormal(x: number, z: number): THREE.Vector3 {
    const eps = 0.25;
    const hL = getGardenTerrainHeight(x - eps, z);
    const hR = getGardenTerrainHeight(x + eps, z);
    const hD = getGardenTerrainHeight(x, z - eps);
    const hU = getGardenTerrainHeight(x, z + eps);
    return new THREE.Vector3(hL - hR, 2 * eps, hD - hU).normalize();
}

/**
 * Creates an artistically faceted low-poly mesh with welded vertices
 * and intentional flat-shaded facets.
 */
export function createSculptedFacetedGeometry(
    radius: number,
    detail: number,
    scaleXYZ: [number, number, number],
    jitterAmount: number,
    seedOffset: number,
    flattenBottom = false
): THREE.BufferGeometry {
    const baseGeo = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(radius, detail);
    const geo = baseGeo.toNonIndexed();
    const pos = geo.attributes.position;

    const vertexMap = new Map<string, [number, number, number]>();

    for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
        const vx = pos.getX(i);
        const vy = pos.getY(i);
        const vz = pos.getZ(i);
        const key = `${vx.toFixed(3)}_${vy.toFixed(3)}_${vz.toFixed(3)}`;

        let displaced = vertexMap.get(key);
        if (!displaced) {
            const hash = vx * 19.19 + vy * 37.37 + vz * 73.73 + seedOffset * 13.37;
            const n1 = (seededRandom(hash) - 0.5) * 2 * jitterAmount;
            const n2 = (seededRandom(hash + 7.1) - 0.5) * 2 * jitterAmount;
            const n3 = (seededRandom(hash + 19.3) - 0.5) * 2 * jitterAmount;

            let nx = (vx + n1 * radius) * scaleXYZ[0];
            let ny = (vy + n2 * radius) * scaleXYZ[1];
            let nz = (vz + n3 * radius) * scaleXYZ[2];

            if (flattenBottom && ny < -radius * scaleXYZ[1] * 0.35) {
                ny = -radius * scaleXYZ[1] * 0.38;
            }

            displaced = [nx, ny, nz];
            vertexMap.set(key, displaced);
        }

        pos.setXYZ(i, displaced[0], displaced[1], displaced[2]);
    }

    pos.needsUpdate = true;
    geo.computeVertexNormals();
    return geo;
}
