// src/scenes/Lighting/GardenLighting.tsx
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { THEME } from '../../styles/theme';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';

const SunsetSkyDome: React.FC = () => {
    const domeRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);

    const skyGeo = useMemo(() => {
        const geo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(185, 32, 24);
        const pos = geo.attributes.position;
        const colors = new Float32Array(pos.count * 3);

        const cZenith = new THREE.Color(THEME.colors.skyZenith);
        const cMid = new THREE.Color(THEME.colors.skyMid);
        const cHorizon = new THREE.Color(THEME.colors.skyHorizon);
        const temp = new THREE.Color();

        for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
            const y = pos.getY(i);
            const normY = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(y / 105, 0, 1);

            if (normY < 0.35) {
                temp.copy(cHorizon).lerp(cMid, normY / 0.35);
            } else {
                temp.copy(cMid).lerp(cZenith, (normY - 0.35) / 0.65);
            }

            colors[i * 3] = temp.r;
            colors[i * 3 + 1] = temp.g;
            colors[i * 3 + 2] = temp.b;
        }

        geo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors, 3));
        return geo;
    }, []);

    useFrame(() => {
        if (domeRef.current) {
            domeRef.current.position.set(
                playerTelemetry.position[0],
                0,
                playerTelemetry.position[2]
            );
        }
    });

    return (
        <mesh ref={domeRef} geometry={skyGeo}>
            <meshBasicMaterial vertexColors side={THREE.BackSide} fog={false} />
        </mesh>
    );
};

export const GardenLighting: React.FC = () => {
    const quality = useGameStore((state) => state.quality);
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const introStage = useGameStore((state) => state.introStage);

    const fogRef = useRef<THREE.FogExp2>(null);
    const dirLightRef = useRef<THREE.DirectionalLight>(null);

    const shadowMapSize = quality === 'high' ? 2048 : 1024;

    useFrame((_, delta) => {
        if (fogRef.current) {
            const targetDensity =
                phase === 'loading' && introStage <= 2 ? 0.022 : 0.011;
            fogRef.current.density = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                fogRef.current.density,
                targetDensity,
                delta * 1.8
            );
        }

        // Keep directional shadow frustum centered near the player
        if (dirLightRef.current) {
            const px = playerTelemetry.position[0];
            const pz = playerTelemetry.position[2];
            dirLightRef.current.position.set(px - 28, 18, pz - 36);
            dirLightRef.current.target.position.set(px, 0, pz);
            dirLightRef.current.target.updateMatrixWorld();
        }
    });

    return (
        <>
            <color attach="background" args={[THEME.colors.fogColor]} />
            <fogExp2 ref={fogRef} attach="fog" args={[THEME.colors.fogColor, 0.02]} />

            <SunsetSkyDome />

            <ambientLight intensity={0.75} color="#cfa8b2" />
            <hemisphereLight
                args={[THEME.colors.skyHorizon, THEME.colors.meadowShadow, 0.95]}
            />

            <directionalLight
                ref={dirLightRef}
                position={[-28, 18, -36]}
                intensity={2.15}
                color="#ffd7ad"
                castShadow
                shadow-mapSize-width={shadowMapSize}
                shadow-mapSize-height={shadowMapSize}
                shadow-camera-near={1}
                shadow-camera-far={120}
                shadow-camera-left={-32}
                shadow-camera-right={32}
                shadow-camera-top={32}
                shadow-camera-bottom={-32}
                shadow-bias={-0.0004}
            />

            <directionalLight
                position={[18, 10, 22]}
                intensity={0.55}
                color="#e6b09c"
            />
        </>
    );
};

/**
 * Stylized Reflection Pond with Shoreline Rocks, Reeds & Ripples
 */
export const StylizedReflectionPond: React.FC<WorldAssetProps> = React.memo(
    ({ position = [0, 0, 0], scale = 1 }: WorldAssetProps) => {
    const rippleRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);

    const shoreRocks = useMemo(
        () =>
            [
                [-2.8, 0.15, -1.2, 0.65, 11],
                [-2.2, 0.12, -2.1, 0.48, 22],
                [2.6, 0.18, -1.5, 0.72, 33],
                [2.9, 0.12, 0.8, 0.52, 44],
                [-2.5, 0.14, 1.8, 0.58, 55],
                [1.8, 0.12, 2.3, 0.46, 66],
            ].map(([rx, ry, rz, rScale, seed]) => ({
                pos: [rx, ry, rz] as [number, number, number],
                geo: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(
                    rScale,
                    1,
                    [1.3, 0.65, 1.1],
                    0.2,
                    seed,
                    true
                ),
            })),
        []
    );

    useFrame(({ clock }) => {
        if (rippleRef.current) {
            const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
            const s = 1 + Math.sin(t * 1.4) * 0.04;
            rippleRef.current.scale.set(s, s, 1);
        }
    });

    return (
        <group position={position} scale={scale}>
            {/* Sandy Pond Bed Rim */}
            <mesh position={[0, 0.03, 0]} receiveShadow>
                <cylinderGeometry args={[3.45, 3.65, 0.08, 14]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial color="#b89f86" roughness={0.9} flatShading />
            </mesh>
            {/* Turquoise Reflective Water Surface */}
            <mesh position={[0, 0.08, 0]} receiveShadow>
                <cylinderGeometry args={[3.15, 3.25, 0.06, 14]} />
                <meshStandardMaterial
                    color={ASSET_PALETTE.waterSurface}
                    emissive={ASSET_PALETTE.waterDeep}
                    emissiveIntensity={0.22}
                    roughness={0.18}
                    metalness={0.15}
                    flatShading
                />
            </mesh>
            {/* Subtle Water Surface Ripple Ring */}
            <mesh ref={rippleRef} position={[0.4, 0.115, -0.3]} rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
                <ringGeometry args={[0.85, 0.94, 24]} />
                <meshBasicMaterial
                    color={ASSET_PALETTE.waterFoam}
                    transparent
                    opacity={0.45}
                    side={THREE.DoubleSide}
                />
            </mesh>
            {/* Shoreline Boulders */}
            {shoreRocks.map((r, i) => (
                <mesh key={i} geometry={r.geo} position={r.pos} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.stoneMid} roughness={0.88} flatShading />
                </mesh>
            ))}
            {/* Shoreline Reed Tufts */}
            {[
                [-2.6, 0.1, -0.4],
                [2.4, 0.1, -0.8],
                [-1.9, 0.1, 2.1],
            ].map(([tx, ty, tz], idx) => (
                <group key={`reed-${idx}`} position={[tx, ty, tz]}>
                    {[-0.12, 0, 0.12].map((ox, j) => (
                        <mesh
                            key={j}
                            position={[ox, 0.35, 0]}
                            rotation={[0.08, j * 0.4, (j - 1) * 0.22]}
                        >
                            <coneGeometry args={[0.08, 0.75, 4]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.mossGreen} flatShading />
                        </mesh>
                    ))}
                </group>
            ))}
        </group>
    );
});

/**
 * Wooden Arched Bridge (Curved deck planks, arched stringers & handrails)
 */
export const WoodenArchedBridge: React.FC<WorldAssetProps> = React.memo(
    ({ position = [0, 0, 0], rotationY = 0, scale = 1 }: WorldAssetProps) => {
    const planks = useMemo(() => {
        const count = 13;
        return Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, i) => {
            const u = (i / (count - 1)) * 2 - 1; // -1 .. +1
            const z = u * 2.35;
            const archY = (1 - u * u) * 0.68 + 0.15;
            const pitch = Math.atan2(-2 * u * 0.68, 2.35);
            return { z, archY, pitch };
        });
    }, []);

    return (
        <group position={position} rotation={[0, rotationY, 0]} scale={scale}>
            {/* Curved Deck Planks */}
            {planks.map((p, idx) => (
                <mesh
                    key={`ap-${idx}`}
                    position={[0, p.archY, p.z]}
                    rotation={[p.pitch, 0, 0]}
                    castShadow
                    receiveShadow
                >
                    <boxGeometry args={[1.95, 0.09, 0.34]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={idx % 2 === 0 ? ASSET_PALETTE.woodLight : ASSET_PALETTE.woodMid}
                        roughness={0.84}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>
            ))}

            {/* Side Posts & Arched Handrails */}
            {[-0.92, 0.92].map((rx, sIdx) => (
                <group key={`arail-${sIdx}`}>
                    {[-2.2, -1.1, 0, 1.1, 2.2].map((pz, pIdx) => {
                        const u = pz / 2.35;
                        const py = (1 - u * u) * 0.68 + 0.55;
                        return (
                            <mesh key={`apost-${pIdx}`} position={[rx, py, pz]} castShadow>
                                <boxGeometry args={[0.15, 0.88, 0.15]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodDark} roughness={0.85} flatShading />
                            </mesh>
                        );
                    })}
                    {/* Segmented Arched Top Rail */}
                    {[-1.65, -0.55, 0.55, 1.65].map((rz, rIdx) => {
                        const u = rz / 2.35;
                        const ry = (1 - u * u) * 0.68 + 0.92;
                        const pitch = Math.atan2(-2 * u * 0.68, 2.35);
                        return (
                            <mesh
                                key={`top-seg-${rIdx}`}
                                position={[rx, ry, rz]}
                                rotation={[pitch, 0, 0]}
                                castShadow
                            >
                                <boxGeometry args={[0.1, 0.1, 1.22]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodMid} roughness={0.84} flatShading />
                            </mesh>
                        );
                    })}
                </group>
            ))}
        </group>
    );
});

/**
 * Explorer Campsite (A-Frame Tent, Hanging Peak Lantern & Animated Campfire)
 */
export const ExplorerCampsite: React.FC<WorldAssetProps> = React.memo(
    ({ position = [0, 0, 0], rotationY = 0 }: WorldAssetProps) => {
    const flameRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const fireLightRef = useRef<THREE.PointLight>(null);

    useFrame(({ clock }) => {
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        if (flameRef.current) {
            const s = 1 + Math.sin(t * 7.5) * 0.12;
            flameRef.current.scale.set(1, s, 1);
            flameRef.current.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * 3.2) * 0.25;
        }
        if (fireLightRef.current) {
            fireLightRef.current.intensity = 1.85 + Math.sin(t * 8.2) * 0.35;
        }
    });

    return (
        <group position={position} rotation={[0, rotationY, 0]}>
            {/* 1. A-Frame Canvas Explorer Tent */}
            <group position={[0.85, 0, -0.4]} rotation={[0, -0.35, 0]}>
                {/* Tent Roof Slopes */}
                <mesh position={[-0.62, 0.92, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, 0.58]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <boxGeometry args={[0.06, 2.15, 2.45]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.canvasLight} roughness={0.85} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0.62, 0.92, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, -0.58]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <boxGeometry args={[0.06, 2.15, 2.45]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.canvasMid} roughness={0.86} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                {/* Crossed Front & Back Timber Tent Poles */}
                {[1.25, -1.25].map((pz, idx) => (
                    <group key={`poles-${idx}`} position={[0, 0, pz]}>
                        <mesh position={[-0.55, 1.02, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, -0.54]} castShadow>
                            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.04, 0.05, 2.35, 6]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodDark} />
                        </mesh>
                        <mesh position={[0.55, 1.02, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, 0.54]} castShadow>
                            <cylinderGeometry args={[0.04, 0.05, 2.35, 6]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodDark} />
                        </mesh>
                    </group>
                ))}
                {/* Ridge Pole & Hanging Tent Lantern */}
                <mesh position={[0, 1.82, 0]} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]} castShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.045, 0.045, 2.75, 6]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodDark} />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 1.52, 1.32]}>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.08, 0.065, 0.18, 6]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={ASSET_PALETTE.lanternCore}
                        emissive={ASSET_PALETTE.lanternGlow}
                        emissiveIntensity={2.0}
                    />
                </mesh>
            </group>

            {/* 2. Stone-Ringed Campfire with Animated Low-Poly Flame */}
            <group position={[-0.95, 0, 1.15]}>
                {/* Ring of 8 Campfire Stones */}
                {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => {
                    const a = (i / 8) * Math.PI * 2;
                    return (
                        <mesh
                            key={`fstone-${i}`}
                            position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.46, 0.09, Math.sin(a) * 0.46]}
                            rotation={[0, a, 0]}
                            castShadow
                            receiveShadow
                        >
                            <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.14, 0]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.stoneDark} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
                        </mesh>
                    );
                })}
                {/* Crossed Firewood Logs */}
                {[0, 1, 2].map((l) => (
                    <mesh
                        key={`flog-${l}`}
                        position={[0, 0.1, 0]}
                        rotation={[Math.PI / 2 - 0.25, (l * Math.PI) / 3, 0]}
                        castShadow
                    >
                        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.06, 0.07, 0.68, 6]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodDeep} roughness={0.9} flatShading />
                    </mesh>
                ))}
                {/* Flickering Multi-Faceted Flame */}
                <group ref={flameRef} position={[0, 0.14, 0]}>
                    <mesh position={[0, 0.22, 0]}>
                        <coneGeometry args={[0.22, 0.52, 6]} />
                        <meshBasicMaterial color="#ff9d2e" />
                    </mesh>
                    <mesh position={[0, 0.18, 0]}>
                        <coneGeometry args={[0.13, 0.36, 5]} />
                        <meshBasicMaterial color="#fff3b8" />
                    </mesh>
                </group>
                <pointLight
                    ref={fireLightRef}
                    position={[0, 0.55, 0]}
                    color="#ffaa42"
                    intensity={1.85}
                    distance={6.8}
                    decay={2}
                />
            </group>

            {/* Companion Barrel, Crate & Tree Stump */}
            <group position={[-2.25, 0, -0.35]}>
                {/* Wooden Barrel */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.36, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.26, 0.24, 0.72, 9]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodMid} roughness={0.84} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                {/* Wooden Crate */}
                <mesh position={[-0.58, 0.25, 0.18]} rotation={[0, 0.28, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <boxGeometry args={[0.5, 0.5, 0.5]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodLight} roughness={0.84} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                {/* Tree Stump */}
                <mesh position={[0.72, 0.2, 0.25]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.22, 0.36, 0.4, 7]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.woodDark} roughness={0.88} flatShading />
                </mesh>
            </group>
        </group>
    );
});

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * 5. AMBIENT DETAILS: SPOTTED MUSHROOMS & GLOWING BUTTERFLIES
 * ============================================================================
 */

export const SpottedMushroomCluster: React.FC<WorldAssetProps> = React.memo(
    ({ position = [0, 0, 0], scale = 1 }: WorldAssetProps) => (
    <group position={position} scale={scale}>
        {[
            [0, 0, 0, 1.0],
            [-0.18, 0, 0.12, 0.65],
        ].map(([mx, my, mz, ms], i) => (
            <group key={i} position={[mx, my, mz]} scale={ms}>
                <mesh position={[0, 0.11, 0]} castShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.045, 0.065, 0.22, 7]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.mushroomCream} roughness={0.85} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 0.24, 0]} castShadow>
                    <coneGeometry args={[0.16, 0.15, 8]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={ASSET_PALETTE.mushroomRed} roughness={0.78} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                {/* White Toadstool Spots */}
                {[
                    [0.06, 0.25, 0.06],
                    [-0.07, 0.24, 0.05],
                    [0, 0.28, -0.06],
                ].map(([sx, sy, sz], j) => (
                    <mesh key={j} position={[sx, sy, sz]}>
                        <sphereGeometry args={[0.022, 5, 5]} />
                        <meshBasicMaterial color="#fff9ee" />
                    </mesh>
                ))}
            </group>
        ))}
    </group>
));

export const GlowingButterflies: React.FC<GlowingButterfliesProps> = React.memo(
    ({ center = [0, 1.4, 0], count = 8 }: GlowingButterfliesProps) => {
    const groupRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);

    const butterflies = useMemo(
        () =>
            Array.from({ length: count }).map((_, i) => ({
                radius: 1.5 + seededRandom(i * 17) * 5.5,
                speed: 0.45 + seededRandom(i * 29) * 0.45,
                phase: seededRandom(i * 43) * Math.PI * 2,
                yOffset: 0.8 + seededRandom(i * 61) * 1.8,
            })),
        [count]
    );

    useFrame(({ clock }) => {
        if (!groupRef.current) return;
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
        groupRef.current.children.forEach((child, idx) => {
            const b = butterflies[idx];
            const angle = t * b.speed + b.phase;
            child.position.set(
                center[0] + Math.cos(angle) * b.radius,
                center[1] + b.yOffset + Math.sin(t * 2.4 + b.phase) * 0.35,
                center[2] + Math.sin(angle * 0.8) * b.radius
            );
            child.rotation.y = -angle + Math.PI / 2;
            const leftWing = child.children[0];
            const rightWing = child.children[1];
            const flap = Math.sin(t * 14 + b.phase) * 0.65;
            if (leftWing) leftWing.rotation.z = flap;
            if (rightWing) rightWing.rotation.z = -flap;
        });
    });

    return (
        <group ref={groupRef}>
            {butterflies.map((_, i) => (
                <group key={i}>
                    <mesh position={[-0.045, 0, 0]} scale={[1.2, 0.15, 0.85]}>
                        <octahedronGeometry args={[0.05, 0]} />
                        <meshBasicMaterial color="#ffe885" />
                    </mesh>
                    <mesh position={[0.045, 0, 0]} scale={[1.2, 0.15, 0.85]}>
                        <octahedronGeometry args={[0.05, 0]} />
                        <meshBasicMaterial color="#ffe885" />
                    </mesh>
                </group>
            ))}
        </group>
    );
});

================================================================================
FILE #28 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/components/Camera/ThirdPersonCamera.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\components\Camera\ThirdPersonCamera.tsx
FILE TYPE     : .tsx
DESCRIPTION   : Third-person orbit camera with collision detection, spring damping, and mouse look controls.
================================================================================
// src/components/Camera/ThirdPersonCamera.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useFrame, useThree } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import { getGardenTerrainHeight } from '../../scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils';
import { collisionManager } from '../../systems/Collision/collisionSystem';

const MIN_PITCH = -0.22;
const MAX_PITCH = 1.05;
const MIN_DISTANCE = 1.35;
const MAX_DISTANCE = 10.5;

export const ThirdPersonCamera: React.FC = () => {
    const { gl } = useThree();
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const inputMode = useGameStore((state) => state.inputMode);
    const setInputMode = useGameStore((state) => state.setInputMode);
    const activeModal = useGameStore((state) => state.activeModal);

    const currentPivot = useRef(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.56, 7.5));
    const currentLookAt = useRef(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1.56, 3.0));
    const currentDistance = useRef(playerTelemetry.cameraDistance);

    // Synchronize Pointer Lock and Canvas Event Listeners
    useEffect(() => {
        const dom = gl.domElement;

        const requestLock = () => {
            if (document.pointerLockElement !== dom) {
                try {
                    dom.requestPointerLock?.();
                } catch {
                    // Browser may defer until user gesture
                }
            }
        };

        const releaseLock = () => {
            if (document.pointerLockElement === dom) {
                try {
                    document.exitPointerLock?.();
                } catch {
                    // Ignore
                }
            }
        };

        // When entering exploring phase in Camera Mode, automatically request lock
        if (phase === 'exploring' && inputMode === 'camera' && activeModal === 'none') {
            requestLock();
        } else {
            releaseLock();
        }

        // Left clicking the 3D canvas while in cursor mode immediately returns to camera mode
        const onCanvasClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            const state = useGameStore.getState();
            if (state.phase !== 'exploring' || state.activeModal !== 'none') return;

            if (state.inputMode === 'cursor') {
                e.preventDefault();
                state.setInputMode('camera');
                requestLock();
            } else {
                requestLock();
            }
        };

        // Toggle between Camera Mode & Cursor Mode using Alt or Escape
        const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.repeat) return;
            const state = useGameStore.getState();
            if (state.phase !== 'exploring') return;

            if (e.key === 'Alt' || e.code === 'AltLeft' || e.code === 'AltRight') {
                e.preventDefault();
                // Toggle input mode cleanly
                state.toggleInputMode();
            } else if (e.key === 'Escape' && state.inputMode === 'camera') {
                e.preventDefault();
                state.setInputMode('cursor');
            }
        };

        const onPointerLockChange = () => {
            const locked = document.pointerLockElement === dom;
            const state = useGameStore.getState();
            // If the browser exited pointer lock via Escape, ensure state switches to cursor mode
            if (!locked && state.inputMode === 'camera' && state.activeModal === 'none') {
                state.setInputMode('cursor');
            }
        };

        // Camera Rotation: Strictly active in MODE A (Camera Mode), frozen in MODE B (Cursor Mode)
        const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            const state = useGameStore.getState();
            if (
                state.phase !== 'exploring' ||
                state.inputMode !== 'camera' ||
                state.activeModal !== 'none'
            ) {
                return; // Zero camera movement while in cursor mode
            }

            const sensitivity = 0.0028;
            const dx = e.movementX;
            const dy = e.movementY;

            // Filter extreme browser pointer attach spikes
            if (Math.abs(dx) > 300 || Math.abs(dy) > 300) return;

            playerTelemetry.cameraYaw -= dx * sensitivity;
            playerTelemetry.cameraPitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
                playerTelemetry.cameraPitch + dy * sensitivity,
                MIN_PITCH,
                MAX_PITCH
            );
        };

        const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {
            const state = useGameStore.getState();
            if (state.phase !== 'exploring' || state.inputMode !== 'camera') return;
            playerTelemetry.cameraDistance = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
                playerTelemetry.cameraDistance + e.deltaY * 0.004,
                2.6,
                MAX_DISTANCE
            );
        };

        dom.addEventListener('click', onCanvasClick);
        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
        document.addEventListener('pointerlockchange', onPointerLockChange);
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
        dom.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: true });

        return () => {
            dom.removeEventListener('click', onCanvasClick);
            window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
            document.removeEventListener('pointerlockchange', onPointerLockChange);
            window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
            dom.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);
        };
    }, [gl, phase, inputMode, activeModal, setInputMode]);

    useFrame((state, rawDelta) => {
        if (phase !== 'exploring') return;
        const delta = Math.min(rawDelta, 0.05);

        const [px, py, pz] = playerTelemetry.position;

        // Follow character pivot smoothly
        const desiredPivot = new THREE.Vector3(px, py + 1.56, pz);
        currentPivot.current.lerp(desiredPivot, Math.min(1, delta * 13));

        const yaw = playerTelemetry.cameraYaw;
        const pitch = playerTelemetry.cameraPitch;
        const desiredDist = playerTelemetry.cameraDistance;

        const offsetDir = new THREE.Vector3(
            Math.sin(yaw) * Math.cos(pitch),
            Math.sin(pitch),
            Math.cos(yaw) * Math.cos(pitch)
        ).normalize();

        const idealCamPos = currentPivot.current
            .clone()
            .addScaledVector(offsetDir, desiredDist);

        // Multi-Probe Collision handling (whiskers against obstacles)
        const rightWhisker = new THREE.Vector3(
            Math.cos(yaw),
            0,
            -Math.sin(yaw)
        ).multiplyScalar(0.28);
        const upWhisker = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.25, 0);

        const fCenter = collisionManager.computeSafeCameraFraction(
            currentPivot.current,
            idealCamPos,
            0.42
        );
        const fLeft = collisionManager.computeSafeCameraFraction(
            currentPivot.current.clone().sub(rightWhisker),
            idealCamPos.clone().sub(rightWhisker),
            0.32
        );
        const fRight = collisionManager.computeSafeCameraFraction(
            currentPivot.current.clone().add(rightWhisker),
            idealCamPos.clone().add(rightWhisker),
            0.32
        );
        const fUp = collisionManager.computeSafeCameraFraction(
            currentPivot.current.clone().add(upWhisker),
            idealCamPos.clone().add(upWhisker),
            0.32
        );

        const safeFraction = Math.min(
            fCenter,
            (fCenter + Math.min(fLeft, fRight, fUp)) * 0.5
        );
        let targetSafeDist = Math.max(MIN_DISTANCE, desiredDist * safeFraction);

        // Prevent ground clipping
        for (const sampleT of [0.35, 0.68, 1.0]) {
            const testPos = currentPivot.current
                .clone()
                .addScaledVector(offsetDir, targetSafeDist * sampleT);
            const groundH = getGardenTerrainHeight(testPos.x, testPos.z) + 0.52;
            if (testPos.y < groundH && sampleT === 1.0) {
                const dyNeeded = groundH - currentPivot.current.y;
                if (offsetDir.y < 0.05) {
                    targetSafeDist = Math.max(MIN_DISTANCE, targetSafeDist * 0.78);
                } else {
                    targetSafeDist = Math.max(
                        MIN_DISTANCE,
                        Math.min(targetSafeDist, dyNeeded / Math.max(0.1, offsetDir.y))
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        const distLerpSpeed =
            targetSafeDist < currentDistance.current ? 18.5 : 4.8;
        currentDistance.current = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
            currentDistance.current,
            targetSafeDist,
            Math.min(1, delta * distLerpSpeed)
        );

        const finalCamPos = currentPivot.current
            .clone()
            .addScaledVector(offsetDir, currentDistance.current);

        const camGroundFloor =
            getGardenTerrainHeight(finalCamPos.x, finalCamPos.z) + 0.46;
        if (finalCamPos.y < camGroundFloor) {
            finalCamPos.y = camGroundFloor;
        }

        state.camera.position.copy(finalCamPos);

        const forwardAim = new THREE.Vector3(
            -Math.sin(yaw) * 1.45,
            0.22,
            -Math.cos(yaw) * 1.45
        );
        const desiredLook = currentPivot.current.clone().add(forwardAim);
        currentLookAt.current.lerp(desiredLook, Math.min(1, delta * 15));
        state.camera.lookAt(currentLookAt.current);
    });

    return null;
};

================================================================================
FILE #29 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/components/Character/PlayerCharacter.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\components\Character\PlayerCharacter.tsx
FILE TYPE     : .tsx
DESCRIPTION   : Player movement physics controller handling keyboard input, sprint, jump, gravity, and orientation.
================================================================================
// src/components/Character/PlayerCharacter.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import {
    getGardenTerrainHeight,
    getGardenTerrainNormal,
} from '../../scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils';
import { collisionManager } from '../../systems/Collision/collisionSystem';
import { ExplorerModelMesh, type ExplorerRigRefs } from './ExplorerModelMesh';

const GRAVITY = -22.0;
const JUMP_VELOCITY = 7.8;
const WALK_SPEED = 4.6;
const RUN_SPEED = 8.2;
const ACCELERATION = 11.5;
const DECELERATION = 14.0;

export const PlayerCharacter: React.FC = () => {
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const activeModal = useGameStore((state) => state.activeModal);
    const characterModel = useGameStore((state) => state.characterModel);

    const rootRef = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);

    // Articulated Rig References for ExplorerModelMesh
    const bodyGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const headGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const ponchoGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const scarfTailGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const backpackGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const lanternGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const lanternLight = useRef<THREE.PointLight>(null);
    const leftArmGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const rightArmGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const leftLegGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);
    const rightLegGroup = useRef<THREE.Group>(null);

    const rigRefs: ExplorerRigRefs = {
        bodyGroup,
        headGroup,
        ponchoGroup,
        scarfTailGroup,
        backpackGroup,
        lanternGroup,
        lanternLight,
        leftArmGroup,
        rightArmGroup,
        leftLegGroup,
        rightLegGroup,
    };

    const keys = useRef<{ [key: string]: boolean }>({});
    const velocity = useRef(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0));
    const animCycle = useRef(0);

    useEffect(() => {
        const startY = getGardenTerrainHeight(
            playerTelemetry.position[0],
            playerTelemetry.position[2]
        );
        playerTelemetry.position[1] = startY;
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            keys.current[e.code] = true;

            if (
                e.code === 'Space' &&
                playerTelemetry.isGrounded &&
                phase === 'exploring' &&
                activeModal === 'none'
            ) {
                velocity.current.y = JUMP_VELOCITY;
                playerTelemetry.isGrounded = false;
            }
        };

        const onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            keys.current[e.code] = false;
        };

        const onBlur = () => {
            keys.current = {};
        };

        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
        window.addEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
        window.addEventListener('blur', onBlur);
        return () => {
            window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
            window.removeEventListener('keyup', onKeyUp);
            window.removeEventListener('blur', onBlur);
        };
    }, [phase, activeModal]);

    useFrame((_, rawDelta) => {
        const delta = Math.min(rawDelta, 0.05);
        if (!rootRef.current) return;

        const canMove = phase === 'exploring' && activeModal === 'none';

        // 1. Camera-Relative Movement Vector
        let inputForward = 0;
        let inputRight = 0;
        if (canMove) {
            if (keys.current['KeyW'] || keys.current['ArrowUp']) inputForward += 1;
            if (keys.current['KeyS'] || keys.current['ArrowDown']) inputForward -= 1;
            if (keys.current['KeyD'] || keys.current['ArrowRight']) inputRight += 1;
            if (keys.current['KeyA'] || keys.current['ArrowLeft']) inputRight -= 1;
        }

        const inputLen = Math.hypot(inputForward, inputRight);
        const isSprinting =
            keys.current['ShiftLeft'] || keys.current['ShiftRight'];
        const targetSpeed =
            inputLen > 0 ? (isSprinting ? RUN_SPEED : WALK_SPEED) : 0;

        const camYaw = playerTelemetry.cameraYaw;
        const forwardVec = new THREE.Vector3(
            -Math.sin(camYaw),
            0,
            -Math.cos(camYaw)
        );
        const rightVec = new THREE.Vector3(
            Math.cos(camYaw),
            0,
            -Math.sin(camYaw)
        );

        const moveDir = new THREE.Vector3();
        if (inputLen > 0.001) {
            moveDir
                .addScaledVector(forwardVec, inputForward / inputLen)
                .addScaledVector(rightVec, inputRight / inputLen)
                .normalize();
        }

        // 2. Smooth Acceleration, Deceleration & Slope Handling
        const [curX, curY, curZ] = playerTelemetry.position;
        const surfaceNormal = getGardenTerrainNormal(curX, curZ);
        const slopeFactor = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(surfaceNormal.y, 0.65, 1.0);

        const desiredVelX = moveDir.x * targetSpeed * slopeFactor;
        const desiredVelZ = moveDir.z * targetSpeed * slopeFactor;

        const accelRate = inputLen > 0 ? ACCELERATION : DECELERATION;
        velocity.current.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
            velocity.current.x,
            desiredVelX,
            Math.min(1, delta * accelRate)
        );
        velocity.current.z = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
            velocity.current.z,
            desiredVelZ,
            Math.min(1, delta * accelRate)
        );

        // 3. Gravity & Jump Physics
        velocity.current.y += GRAVITY * delta;

        let nextX = curX + velocity.current.x * delta;
        let nextZ = curZ + velocity.current.z * delta;
        let nextY = curY + velocity.current.y * delta;

        // 4. Obstacle Collision Resolution
        [nextX, nextZ] = collisionManager.resolvePlayerMovement(
            nextX,
            nextY,
            nextZ,
            0.42
        );

        // 5. Ground Clamping & Downhill Slope Adherence
        const groundHeight = getGardenTerrainHeight(nextX, nextZ);
        if (nextY <= groundHeight + 0.03 && velocity.current.y <= 0) {
            nextY = groundHeight;
            velocity.current.y = 0;
            playerTelemetry.isGrounded = true;
        } else if (
            playerTelemetry.isGrounded &&
            nextY - groundHeight < 0.38 &&
            velocity.current.y <= 0
        ) {
            nextY = groundHeight;
            velocity.current.y = 0;
        } else {
            playerTelemetry.isGrounded = false;
        }

        playerTelemetry.position = [nextX, nextY, nextZ];
        playerTelemetry.velocity = [
            velocity.current.x,
            velocity.current.y,
            velocity.current.z,
        ];

        // 6. Natural Shortest-Arc Turning
        const horizSpeed = Math.hypot(velocity.current.x, velocity.current.z);
        if (horizSpeed > 0.2) {
            const targetAngle = Math.atan2(velocity.current.x, velocity.current.z);
            let angleDiff = targetAngle - playerTelemetry.rotationY;
            while (angleDiff > Math.PI) angleDiff -= Math.PI * 2;
            while (angleDiff < -Math.PI) angleDiff += Math.PI * 2;
            playerTelemetry.rotationY += angleDiff * Math.min(1, delta * 12.5);
        }

        rootRef.current.position.set(nextX, nextY, nextZ);
        rootRef.current.rotation.y = playerTelemetry.rotationY;

        // 7. Articulated Rig Animation (Walk, Sprint, Jump, Scarf/Lantern Physics)
        if (!playerTelemetry.isGrounded) {
            playerTelemetry.moveState = 'jump';
        } else if (horizSpeed > 5.8) {
            playerTelemetry.moveState = 'run';
        } else if (horizSpeed > 0.22) {
            playerTelemetry.moveState = 'walk';
        } else {
            playerTelemetry.moveState = 'idle';
        }

        const cycleSpeed =
            playerTelemetry.moveState === 'run'
                ? 13.8
                : playerTelemetry.moveState === 'walk'
                    ? 8.6
                    : 2.0;
        animCycle.current += delta * cycleSpeed;
        const c = animCycle.current;
        const stride = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(horizSpeed / RUN_SPEED, 0, 1);

        if (
            leftLegGroup.current &&
            rightLegGroup.current &&
            leftArmGroup.current &&
            rightArmGroup.current &&
            bodyGroup.current &&
            ponchoGroup.current &&
            scarfTailGroup.current &&
            backpackGroup.current &&
            lanternGroup.current &&
            headGroup.current
        ) {
            if (!playerTelemetry.isGrounded) {
                // Expressive Jump / Airborne Pose
                leftLegGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    leftLegGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0.48,
                    delta * 10
                );
                rightLegGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    rightLegGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    -0.38,
                    delta * 10
                );
                leftArmGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    leftArmGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    -0.45,
                    delta * 10
                );
                rightArmGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    rightArmGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0.35,
                    delta * 10
                );
                scarfTailGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    scarfTailGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0.55,
                    delta * 8
                );
                lanternGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    lanternGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0.38,
                    delta * 8
                );
            } else if (horizSpeed > 0.18) {
                // Grounded Walk & Sprint Stride
                const legSwing = Math.sin(c) * (0.58 * stride + 0.14);
                leftLegGroup.current.rotation.x = legSwing;
                rightLegGroup.current.rotation.x = -legSwing;

                leftArmGroup.current.rotation.x = -legSwing * 0.62;
                rightArmGroup.current.rotation.x = legSwing * 0.62;

                // Vertical gait bounce & forward sprint lean
                bodyGroup.current.position.y = Math.abs(Math.cos(c)) * 0.062 * stride;
                bodyGroup.current.rotation.x = 0.06 + stride * 0.13;
                bodyGroup.current.rotation.z = Math.sin(c) * 0.025 * stride;

                // Poncho, Scarf & Lantern secondary motion
                ponchoGroup.current.rotation.x =
                    0.05 + stride * 0.1 + Math.sin(c * 2) * 0.03;
                scarfTailGroup.current.rotation.x =
                    0.18 + stride * 0.38 + Math.sin(c * 2 + 0.6) * 0.09;
                scarfTailGroup.current.rotation.z = Math.cos(c) * 0.08 * stride;
                backpackGroup.current.position.y =
                    1.07 + Math.sin(c * 2 - 0.5) * 0.018 * stride;
                lanternGroup.current.rotation.x =
                    0.12 * stride + Math.sin(c * 2 - 0.8) * 0.18 * stride;
                lanternGroup.current.rotation.z = Math.cos(c) * 0.14 * stride;
            } else {
                // Gentle Breathing & Looking Idle
                leftLegGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    leftLegGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0,
                    delta * 10
                );
                rightLegGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    rightLegGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0,
                    delta * 10
                );
                leftArmGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    leftArmGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0,
                    delta * 10
                );
                rightArmGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    rightArmGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0,
                    delta * 10
                );
                bodyGroup.current.position.y = Math.sin(c) * 0.016;
                bodyGroup.current.rotation.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    bodyGroup.current.rotation.x,
                    0,
                    delta * 8
                );
                bodyGroup.current.rotation.z = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                    bodyGroup.current.rotation.z,
                    0,
                    delta * 8
                );
                ponchoGroup.current.rotation.x = Math.sin(c) * 0.015;
                scarfTailGroup.current.rotation.x = 0.08 + Math.sin(c * 1.2) * 0.035;
                lanternGroup.current.rotation.x = Math.sin(c * 1.1) * 0.04;
                lanternGroup.current.rotation.z = Math.cos(c * 0.9) * 0.03;
                headGroup.current.rotation.y = Math.sin(c * 0.45) * 0.07;
            }
        }

        // 8. Signature Memory Lantern Feature: "Glows brighter near memories" (Character Model M.png)
        if (lanternLight.current) {
            const memDist = playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryDistance;
            const proximityBoost =
                memDist < 7.0 ? Math.pow(1 - memDist / 7.0, 1.5) * 2.2 : 0;
            lanternLight.current.intensity = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
                lanternLight.current.intensity,
                1.1 + proximityBoost,
                delta * 6
            );
        }
    });

    if (phase !== 'exploring') return null;

    return (
        <group ref={rootRef} position={[0, 0, 7.5]} rotation={[0, Math.PI, 0]}>
            <ExplorerModelMesh archetype={characterModel} rig={rigRefs} />
        </group>
    );
};

================================================================================
END OF FILE: src/components/Character/PlayerCharacter.tsx

    headGroup: React.RefObject<THREE.Group | null>;

    backpackGroup: React.RefObject<THREE.Group | null>;

    rightArmGroup: React.RefObject<THREE.Group | null>;
    leftLegGroup: React.RefObject<THREE.Group | null>;
    rightLegGroup: React.RefObject<THREE.Group | null>;
}

interface ExplorerModelMeshProps {
    archetype: CharacterArchetype;
    rig: ExplorerRigRefs;
}

const PALETTE = {
    skin: '#f3ccb4',

export const ExplorerModelMesh: React.FC<ExplorerModelMeshProps> = React.memo(
    ({ archetype, rig }) => {
        // Sculpted flared poncho cape geometry with wide kimono-style shoulder drape
        const ponchoMainGeo = useMemo(() => {
            const geo = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.22, 0.58, 0.68, 14, 4, true);
            const pos = geo.attributes.position;
            for (let i = 0; i < pos.count; i++) {
                const x = pos.getX(i);
                const y = pos.getY(i);
                const z = pos.getZ(i);
                const bottomFactor = (0.34 - y) / 0.68;
                const angle = Math.atan2(x, z);
                // Create front V-split and wide wing-like shoulder sleeves
                const sleeveFlare = Math.abs(Math.sin(angle)) * 0.14 * bottomFactor;
                const wave = Math.sin(angle * 6) * 0.022 * bottomFactor;
                pos.setXYZ(
                    i,
                    x * (1.14 + bottomFactor * 0.16 + sleeveFlare) + wave,
                    y - Math.abs(x) * 0.06,
                    z * (0.94 + bottomFactor * 0.08) + wave
                );
            }
            geo.computeVertexNormals();
            return geo;
        }, []);

        return (
            <group ref={rig.bodyGroup}>
                {/* ============================================================
                    1. LEGS, WIDE CUFFED CULOTTES & LACED EXPLORER BOOTS
                   ============================================================ */}
                {(['left', 'right'] as const).map((side) => {
                    const isLeft = side === 'left';
                    const legRef = isLeft ? rig.leftLegGroup : rig.rightLegGroup;
                    const xPos = isLeft ? -0.135 : 0.135;

                    return (
                        <group key={side} ref={legRef} position={[xPos, 0.74, 0]}>
                            {/* Wide Dark Chocolate Culotte Trouser Leg */}
                            <mesh position={[0, -0.17, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>

                                        roughness={0.2}
                                        flatShading
                                    />
                                </mesh>
                                <mesh position={[0, -0.075, 0]}>
                                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.068, 0.05, 0.03, 6]} />
                                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.leatherDark} roughness={0.6} flatShading />
                                </mesh>
                                {/* Corked Keepsake Glass Bottle with Glowing Pink Petal */}
                                <group position={[-0.085, -0.085, 0.03]} rotation={[0, 0, 0.15]}>
                                    <mesh>
                                        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.026, 0.028, 0.088, 8]} />
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color="#ffd4dc"
                                            emissive="#ff8da1"
                                            emissiveIntensity={0.85}
                                            transparent
                                            opacity={0.82}
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh position={[0, 0.052, 0]}>
                                        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.016, 0.018, 0.026, 6]} />
                                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.bedrollTan} />
                                    </mesh>
                                </group>
                            </group>
                        )}

                        <pointLight
                            ref={rig.lanternLight}
                            position={[0, -0.15, -0.05]}
                            color={PALETTE.lanternGlow}
                            intensity={1.15}
                            distance={4.5}
                            decay={2}
                        />
                    </group>
                </group>

                {/* ============================================================
                    6. EXPRESSIVE HEAD, FACETED HAIR LOCKS & EXPLORER GOGGLES
                   ============================================================ */}
                <group ref={rig.headGroup} position={[0, 1.56, 0.02]}>
                    <mesh castShadow>
                        <sphereGeometry args={[0.195, 14, 14]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.skin} roughness={0.72} />
                    </mesh>

                    {[-0.072, 0.072].map((ex, i) => (
                        <React.Fragment key={`eye-${i}`}>
                            <mesh position={[ex, -0.01, 0.175]}>
                                <sphereGeometry args={[0.027, 8, 8]} />
                                <meshBasicMaterial color="#2b1d19" />
                            </mesh>
                            <mesh position={[ex + 0.008, 0.003, 0.196]}>
                                <sphereGeometry args={[0.009, 6, 6]} />
                                <meshBasicMaterial color="#fff8eb" />
                            </mesh>
                            <mesh
                                position={[ex * 1.35, -0.05, 0.162]}
                                scale={[1.2, 0.65, 0.4]}
                            >
                                <sphereGeometry args={[0.024, 7, 7]} />
                                <meshBasicMaterial
                                    color={PALETTE.skinBlush}
                                    transparent
                                    opacity={0.65}
                                />
                            </mesh>
                        </React.Fragment>
                    ))}

                    {archetype === 'male' && (
                        <mesh position={[0.115, -0.055, 0.148]} rotation={[0, 0.55, 0.25]}>
                            <boxGeometry args={[0.048, 0.022, 0.008]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.ponchoCream} roughness={0.85} />
                        </mesh>
                    )}

                    {/* Tousled Dark Brown Hair Base & Warm Highlighted Locks */}
                    <mesh position={[0, 0.045, -0.015]} castShadow>
                        <icosahedronGeometry args={[0.214, 2]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.hairDark} roughness={0.84} flatShading />
                    </mesh>
                    {[
                        { pos: [0, 0.12, 0.15], rot: [0.45, 0, 0], scale: [0.9, 1.15, 0.8] },
                        { pos: [-0.09, 0.1, 0.14], rot: [0.4, 0.2, 0.35], scale: [0.85, 1.1, 0.8] },
                        { pos: [0.09, 0.1, 0.14], rot: [0.4, -0.2, -0.35], scale: [0.85, 1.1, 0.8] },
                        { pos: [-0.17, 0.01, 0.06], rot: [0.1, 0, 0.45], scale: [0.8, 1.35, 0.8] },
                        { pos: [0.17, 0.01, 0.06], rot: [0.1, 0, -0.45], scale: [0.8, 1.35, 0.8] },
                        { pos: [-0.14, 0.06, -0.14], rot: [-0.4, 0, 0.4], scale: [0.9, 1.2, 0.9] },
                        { pos: [0.14, 0.06, -0.14], rot: [-0.4, 0, -0.4], scale: [0.9, 1.2, 0.9] },
                        { pos: [0, 0.04, -0.18], rot: [-0.55, 0, 0], scale: [1.1, 1.25, 0.9] },
                    ].map((strand, idx) => (
                        <mesh
                            key={`strand-${idx}`}
                            position={strand.pos as [number, number, number]}
                            rotation={strand.rot as [number, number, number]}
                            scale={strand.scale as [number, number, number]}
                            castShadow
                        >
                            <coneGeometry args={[0.078, 0.22, 5]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial
                                color={idx % 2 === 0 ? PALETTE.hairDark : PALETTE.hairHighlight}
                                roughness={0.82}
                                flatShading
                            />
                        </mesh>
                    ))}

                    {archetype === 'female' ? (
                        <group>
                            <mesh position={[0, 0.04, 0]}>
                                <torusGeometry args={[0.215, 0.022, 8, 20, Math.PI]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.ponchoCream} roughness={0.6} />
                            </mesh>
                            {[-0.21, 0.21].map((hx, idx) => (
                                <group
                                    key={`earcup-${idx}`}
                                    position={[hx, 0.0, 0.01]}
                                    rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 2]}
                                >
                                    <mesh castShadow>
                                        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.068, 0.068, 0.048, 12]} />
                                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.ponchoCream} roughness={0.55} />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh position={[0, idx === 0 ? 0.026 : -0.026, 0]}>
                                        <cylinderGeometry args={[0.036, 0.036, 0.008, 8]} />
                                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.ponchoPattern} roughness={0.4} />
                                    </mesh>
                                </group>
                            ))}
                            <group position={[0.14, 0.02, -0.17]} rotation={[0.2, 0.5, -0.3]}>
                                <mesh>
                                    <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.035, 0]} />
                                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.flowerWhite} />
                                </mesh>
                                <mesh position={[-0.04, -0.08, -0.02]} rotation={[0, 0, -0.35]}>
                                    <boxGeometry args={[0.028, 0.18, 0.01]} />
                                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.scarfRust} />
                                </mesh>
                                <mesh position={[0.04, -0.09, -0.02]} rotation={[0, 0, 0.28]}>
                                    <boxGeometry args={[0.028, 0.18, 0.01]} />
                                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.scarfRust} />
                                </mesh>
                            </group>
                        </group>
                    ) : (
                        /* Explorer Goggles Resting on Forehead */
                        <group position={[0, 0.13, 0.03]} rotation={[-0.42, 0, 0]}>
                            <mesh rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
                                <torusGeometry args={[0.205, 0.022, 7, 18]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial color={PALETTE.leatherDark} roughness={0.8} />
                            </mesh>
                            {[-0.082, 0.082].map((gx, i) => (
                                <group
                                    key={`goggle-${i}`}
                                    position={[gx, 0.01, 0.185]}
                                    rotation={[0, i === 0 ? -0.22 : 0.22, 0]}
                                >
                                    <mesh castShadow>
                                        <boxGeometry args={[0.105, 0.072, 0.042]} />
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color={PALETTE.brassGold}
                                            metalness={0.45}
                                            roughness={0.35}
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                    <mesh position={[0, 0, 0.016]}>
                                        <boxGeometry args={[0.078, 0.048, 0.018]} />
                                        <meshStandardMaterial
                                            color="#e08338"
                                            emissive="#a84c14"
                                            emissiveIntensity={0.4}
                                            roughness={0.2}
                                        />
                                    </mesh>
                                </group>
                            ))}
                        </group>
                    )}
                </group>
            </group>
        );
    }
);

================================================================================
END OF FILE: src/components/Character/ExplorerModelMesh.tsx

  ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS,
  CHUNK_SIZE,
  getGardenTerrainHeight,
} from '../../scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import type { MemoryItem } from '../../systems/MemoryManager/memoryTypes';

  ArchedMemoryPedestal,
  GrandMemoryPortal,
  MinimalMemoryPedestal,
} from './PedestalModels';

export const INITIAL_GARDEN_MEMORIES: MemoryItem[] = [
  {
    id: 'mem-seed-first-step',

      'A compact sandstone memory pedestal beside the first stepping stones. Its floating golden crystal hums softly as the evening breeze stirs the red floral banner.',

    tags: ['Minimal Pedestal', 'Beginning', 'Lantern'],

      floatingPhotoPalette: ['#e8a598', '#ffcf70', '#5d6b46'],

  {

    subtitle: 'Grand Memory Portal',
    date: 'October 14',
    location: 'Eastern Sakura Bough',
    category: 'photo',
    pedestalVariant: 'grand_portal',
    importance: 'core',
    growthStage: 'landmark',
    interactionState: 'dormant',
    people: ['Elena'],
    groupName: 'Sanctuary Chronicles',
    description:
      'The Grand Memory Portal beneath the eastern sakura bough. Late afternoon sunlight filters through the broken stone arch as the golden crystal and its orbit ring illuminate the stepped flagstones.',
    position: [2.85, 0, 3.6],
    tags: ['Grand Portal', 'Sakura', 'Sanctuary'],
    echo: {
      floatingPhotoTitle: 'Under the Eastern Bough',
      floatingPhotoPalette: ['#d9918b', '#ffdf9e', '#68744e'],
      voiceTranscript:
        '"Look how the petals catch the gold right before the sun dips behind the floating island."',
      ambientAudioLabel: 'Rustling Sakura Blossoms (01:42)',
      writtenNote: 'Anchored at the Grand Memory Portal.',
    },
  },
  {
    id: 'mem-painted-valley',
    title: 'Canvas by the Western Arch',
    subtitle: 'Arched Memory Pedestal',
    date: 'September 28',
    location: 'Ancient Archway Overlook',
    category: 'shrine',
    pedestalVariant: 'arched',
    importance: 'milestone',
    growthStage: 'plant',
    interactionState: 'dormant',
    people: ['Kael'],
    groupName: 'Sketchbook Studies',
    description:
      'An Arched Memory Pedestal overlooking the western ridge. A single curved stone arm draped in white blossoms cradles the orbiting golden memory crystal.',
    position: [-3.6, 0, 2.2],
    tags: ['Arched Pedestal', 'Painting', 'Dusk'],
    echo: {
      floatingPhotoTitle: 'Western Arch Watercolour',

    position: [-1.8, 0, -26.0],
    tags: ['Persistent', 'Outer Valley', 'Minimal Pedestal'],
    echo: {
      floatingPhotoTitle: 'Outer Meadow Vista',
      floatingPhotoPalette: ['#9e8191', '#f7cfc4', '#68744e'],
      voiceTranscript:
        '"No matter how far we wander, the garden remembers where every stone was placed."',
      ambientAudioLabel: 'High Meadow Wind',
      writtenNote: 'Tests cross-chunk memory persistence.',
    },
  },
];

const INTERACTION_RADIUS = 4.2;

export const MemoryPedestals: React.FC = () => {
  const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
  const activeChunkCoord = useGameStore((state) => state.activeChunkCoord);
  const persistentMemories = useGameStore((state) => state.persistentMemories);
  const registerPersistentMemories = useGameStore(
    (state) => state.registerPersistentMemories
  );
  const nearbyMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.nearbyMemory);
  const setNearbyMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.setNearbyMemory);
  const openMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.openMemory);
  const activeModal = useGameStore((state) => state.activeModal);
  const activeEchoId = useGameStore((state) => state.activeEchoId);
  const landmarkZones = useGameStore((state) => state.landmarkZones);
  const setNearbyZone = useGameStore((state) => state.setNearbyZone);

  // Independent animation references for Crystal, Orbit Ring, Shards, Ground Ripple, and Light
  const crystalRefs = useRef<Record<string, THREE.Group | null>>({});
  const orbitRingRefs = useRef<Record<string, THREE.Mesh | null>>({});
  const shardsRefs = useRef<Record<string, THREE.Group | null>>({});
  const wellGlowRefs = useRef<Record<string, THREE.Mesh | null>>({});
  const echoWaveRefs = useRef<Record<string, THREE.Mesh | null>>({});
  const lightRefs = useRef<Record<string, THREE.PointLight | null>>({});

  useEffect(() => {
    const initialized = INITIAL_GARDEN_MEMORIES.map((m) => {
      const y = getGardenTerrainHeight(m.position[0], m.position[2]);
      const cx = Math.round(m.position[0] / CHUNK_SIZE);
      const cz = Math.round(m.position[2] / CHUNK_SIZE);
      return {
        ...m,
        position: [m.position[0], y, m.position[2]] as [number, number, number],
        chunkKey: `${cx}_${cz}`,
      };
    });
    registerPersistentMemories(initialized);
  }, [registerPersistentMemories]);

  const activeChunkMemories = useMemo(() => {
    const [acx, acz] = activeChunkCoord;
    return persistentMemories.filter((m) => {
      const mcx = Math.round(m.position[0] / CHUNK_SIZE);
      const mcz = Math.round(m.position[2] / CHUNK_SIZE);
      return (
        Math.abs(mcx - acx) <= ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS &&
        Math.abs(mcz - acz) <= ACTIVE_CHUNK_RADIUS
      );
    });
  }, [persistentMemories, activeChunkCoord]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (
        e.code === 'KeyE' &&
        phase === 'exploring' &&
        activeModal === 'none'
      ) {
        const currentNearby = useGameStore.getState().nearbyMemory;
        if (currentNearby) {
          openMemory(currentNearby);
        }
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
  }, [phase, activeModal, openMemory]);

  useFrame(({ clock }, delta) => {
    const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
    const [px, , pz] = playerTelemetry.position;

    let closestMemory: MemoryItem | null = null;
    let closestDist = INTERACTION_RADIUS;
    let absoluteNearestDist = 999;

    for (let i = 0; i < activeChunkMemories.length; i++) {
      const mem = activeChunkMemories[i];
      const dx = px - mem.position[0];
      const dz = pz - mem.position[2];
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dz);

      if (dist < absoluteNearestDist) {
        absoluteNearestDist = dist;
      }

      if (phase === 'exploring' && dist < closestDist) {
        closestDist = dist;
        closestMemory = mem;
      }

      const isNear = nearbyMemory?.id === mem.id;
      const isEchoing = activeEchoId === mem.id;

      // 1. Animate Floating Crystal
      const crystal = crystalRefs.current[mem.id];
      if (crystal) {
        crystal.position.y = Math.sin(t * (isNear ? 2.8 : 1.6) + i) * 0.09;
        crystal.rotation.y += delta * (isEchoing ? 1.6 : isNear ? 1.05 : 0.45);
      }

      // 2. Animate Golden Orbit Ring
      const ring = orbitRingRefs.current[mem.id];
      if (ring) {
        ring.rotation.z += delta * (isNear ? 1.2 : 0.5);
        const s = isNear ? 1.08 + Math.sin(t * 3.5) * 0.04 : 1.0;
        ring.scale.set(s, s, s);
      }

      // 3. Animate Floating Diamond Shards
      const shards = shardsRefs.current[mem.id];
      if (shards) {
        shards.rotation.y -= delta * (isNear ? 0.85 : 0.35);
        shards.position.y = Math.cos(t * 2.2 + i) * 0.05;
      }

      // 4. Animate Ground Acoustic Ripple Wave
      const echoWave = echoWaveRefs.current[mem.id];
      if (echoWave) {
        const pulse = (t * 0.8 + i * 0.3) % 1;
        const s = 1 + pulse * (isEchoing ? 2.5 : 1.45);
        echoWave.scale.set(s, s, 1);
        const mat = echoWave.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
        mat.opacity = (1 - pulse) * (isEchoing ? 0.72 : isNear ? 0.42 : 0.18);
      }

      // 5. Dynamic Point Light Intensity
      const light = lightRefs.current[mem.id];
      if (light) {
        const targetIntensity = isEchoing ? 3.8 : isNear ? 2.95 : 1.5;
        light.intensity = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(
          light.intensity,
          targetIntensity,
          delta * 6
        );
      }
    }

    playerTelemetry.nearestMemoryDistance = absoluteNearestDist;

    if (phase === 'exploring') {
      setNearbyMemory(closestMemory);

      let activeZone = null;
      for (const z of landmarkZones) {
        const d = Math.hypot(px - z.position[0], pz - z.position[2]);
        if (d <= z.radius) {
          activeZone = z;
          break;
        }
      }
      setNearbyZone(activeZone);
    }
  });

  if (phase !== 'exploring') return null;

  return (
    <group>
      {activeChunkMemories.map((p, idx) => {
        const isNear = nearbyMemory?.id === p.id;
        const isEchoing = activeEchoId === p.id;
        const variant =
          p.pedestalVariant ||
          (idx % 3 === 0
            ? 'grand_portal'
            : idx % 3 === 1
              ? 'arched'
              : 'minimal');

        const animRefs = {
          crystalRef: (el: THREE.Group | null) => {
            crystalRefs.current[p.id] = el;
          },
          orbitRingRef: (el: THREE.Mesh | null) => {
            orbitRingRefs.current[p.id] = el;
          },
          shardsRef: (el: THREE.Group | null) => {
            shardsRefs.current[p.id] = el;
          },
          wellGlowRef: (el: THREE.Mesh | null) => {
            wellGlowRefs.current[p.id] = el;
          },
        };

        return (
          <group
            key={p.id}
            position={p.position}
            rotation={[0, p.rotationY || 0, 0]}
          >
            {variant === 'grand_portal' && (
              <GrandMemoryPortal
                isNear={isNear}
                isEchoing={isEchoing}
                animRefs={animRefs}
              />
            )}
            {variant === 'minimal' && (
              <MinimalMemoryPedestal
                isNear={isNear}
                isEchoing={isEchoing}
                animRefs={animRefs}
              />
            )}
            {variant === 'arched' && (
              <ArchedMemoryPedestal
                isNear={isNear}
                isEchoing={isEchoing}
                animRefs={animRefs}
              />
            )}

            {/* Expanding Ground Memory Pulse Ring */}
            <mesh
              ref={(el) => {
                echoWaveRefs.current[p.id] = el;
              }}
              position={[0, 0.06, 0]}
              rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
            >
              <ringGeometry args={[0.95, 1.08, 32]} />
              <meshBasicMaterial
                color={THEME.colors.memoryGold}
                transparent
                opacity={0.32}
                side={THREE.DoubleSide}
              />
            </mesh>

            {/* Warm Golden Point Light */}
            <pointLight
              ref={(el) => {
                lightRefs.current[p.id] = el;
              }}
              position={[0, 2.0, 0.2]}
              color={THEME.colors.memoryGold}
              intensity={1.5}
              distance={7.5}
              decay={2}
            />
          </group>
        );
      })}
    </group>
  );
};

END OF FILE: src/components/Memory/MemoryPedestals.tsx
