DESCRIPTION   : 3D memory shrine models, floating crystal geometries, orbital rings, and blooming flower clusters.
================================================================================
// src/components/Memory/PedestalModels.tsx
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { createSculptedFacetedGeometry } from '../../scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils';

export interface PedestalAnimRefs {
    crystalRef: (el: THREE.Group | null) => void;
    orbitRingRef: (el: THREE.Mesh | null) => void;
    shardsRef: (el: THREE.Group | null) => void;
    wellGlowRef: (el: THREE.Mesh | null) => void;
}

interface PedestalModelProps {
    isNear: boolean;
    isEchoing: boolean;
    animRefs?: PedestalAnimRefs;
}

const PEDESTAL_PALETTE = {
    sandstoneLight: '#d8b298',
    sandstoneMid: '#bf967c',
    sandstoneDark: '#99735c',
    sandstoneDeep: '#785745',
    terracottaStone: '#c98e70',
    bannerRed: '#c84b31',
    bannerDark: '#9e3620',
    emblemGold: '#f5b851',
    crystalCore: '#fff3c4',
    crystalMid: '#ffaa33',
    crystalDeep: '#e67e17',
    orbitGold: '#ffdf85',
    wellGlow: '#ffcf66',
    vineGreen: '#5e7840',
    vineLight: '#7b9652',
    flowerWhite: '#fcf5e8',
    flowerCenter: '#e89d48',
};

/**
 * Shared 5-Petal White Keepsake Blossom with Golden Center & Leaves
 */
const PedestalFlowerCluster: React.FC<{
    position: [number, number, number];
    rotation?: [number, number, number];
    scale?: number;
}> = ({ position, rotation = [0, 0, 0], scale = 1 }) => (
    <group position={position} rotation={rotation} scale={scale}>
        <mesh>
            <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.045, 0]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.flowerCenter} flatShading />
        </mesh>
        {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((p) => {
            const a = (p / 5) * Math.PI * 2;
            return (
                <mesh
                    key={p}
                    position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.085, 0.005, Math.sin(a) * 0.085]}
                    rotation={[0, -a, 0]}
                    scale={[1.3, 0.3, 0.85]}
                >
                    <octahedronGeometry args={[0.055, 0]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.flowerWhite}
                        roughness={0.65}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>
            );
        })}
    </group>
);

/**
 * Shared 3D Faceted Bipyramidal Memory Crystal, Orbit Ring & Floating Shards
 */
const FloatingMemoryCrystalAssembly: React.FC<{
    position: [number, number, number];
    hasOrbitRing: boolean;
    ringTilt?: [number, number, number];
    isNear: boolean;
    isEchoing: boolean;
    animRefs?: PedestalAnimRefs;
}> = ({
    position,
    hasOrbitRing,
    ringTilt = [0.38, 0.15, -0.42],
    isNear,
    isEchoing,
    animRefs,
}) => {
        const emissiveBoost = isEchoing ? 2.6 : isNear ? 2.1 : 1.35;

        return (
            <group position={position}>
                {/* Main Floating Faceted Bipyramid Crystal */}
                <group ref={animRefs?.crystalRef}>
                    {/* Upper Faceted Pyramid */}
                    <mesh position={[0, 0.24, 0]} castShadow>
                        <coneGeometry args={[0.26, 0.48, 6]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalCore}
                            emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalMid}
                            emissiveIntensity={emissiveBoost}
                            roughness={0.18}
                            metalness={0.1}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                    {/* Lower Inverted Faceted Pyramid */}
                    <mesh position={[0, -0.22, 0]} rotation={[Math.PI, Math.PI / 6, 0]} castShadow>
                        <coneGeometry args={[0.26, 0.44, 6]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalMid}
                            emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalDeep}
                            emissiveIntensity={emissiveBoost * 0.9}
                            roughness={0.22}
                            metalness={0.1}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                </group>

                {/* Delicate Tilted Golden Orbit Ring */}
                {hasOrbitRing && (
                    <mesh ref={animRefs?.orbitRingRef} rotation={ringTilt}>
                        <torusGeometry args={[0.48, 0.018, 8, 36]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.orbitGold}
                            emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.wellGlow}
                            emissiveIntensity={1.6}
                            roughness={0.2}
                        />
                    </mesh>
                )}

                {/* Floating Magical Diamond Shards & Vertical Light Accents */}
                <group ref={animRefs?.shardsRef}>
                    {[
                        [-0.38, 0.32, 0.12, 0.055],
                        [0.36, -0.18, 0.14, 0.048],
                        [-0.28, -0.32, -0.12, 0.042],
                        [0.24, 0.42, -0.1, 0.05],
                    ].map(([sx, sy, sz, s], i) => (
                        <mesh key={i} position={[sx, sy, sz]} scale={[0.7, 1.35, 0.7]}>
                            <octahedronGeometry args={[s, 0]} />
                            <meshBasicMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.orbitGold} />
                        </mesh>
                    ))}
                </group>
            </group>
        );
    };

/**
 *Shared Orange-Red Hanging Fabric Banner with 4-Petal Golden Floral Emblem
 */
const HangingBannerWithEmblem: React.FC<{
    position: [number, number, number];
    rotation?: [number, number, number];
    scale?: number;
}> = ({ position, rotation = [0, 0, 0], scale = 1 }) => (
    <group position={position} rotation={rotation} scale={scale}>
        {/* Upper Banner Fold over Stone Ledge */}
        <mesh position={[0, 0.04, -0.06]} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]} castShadow>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.32, 0.14, 0.035]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.bannerDark} roughness={0.85} flatShading />
        </mesh>
        {/* Main Hanging Cloth Body */}
        <mesh position={[0, -0.28, 0]} castShadow>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.32, 0.56, 0.03]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.bannerRed} roughness={0.82} flatShading />
        </mesh>
        {/* Angled Swallowtail Bottom Tips */}
        <mesh position={[-0.08, -0.6, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 4]} castShadow>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.14, 0.14, 0.028]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.bannerRed} roughness={0.82} flatShading />
        </mesh>
        <mesh position={[0.08, -0.6, 0]} rotation={[0, 0, Math.PI / 4]} castShadow>
            <boxGeometry args={[0.14, 0.14, 0.028]} />
            <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.bannerRed} roughness={0.82} flatShading />
        </mesh>
        {/* 4-Petal Golden Floral Emblem on Banner Face */}
        <group position={[0, -0.34, 0.02]}>
            {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((p) => {
                const a = (p / 4) * Math.PI * 2;
                return (
                    <mesh
                        key={p}
                        position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.055, Math.sin(a) * 0.055, 0]}
                        rotation={[0, 0, a + Math.PI / 4]}
                        scale={[1, 0.55, 0.3]}
                    >
                        <octahedronGeometry args={[0.042, 0]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.emblemGold}
                            emissive="#d98a2b"
                            emissiveIntensity={0.35}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                );
            })}
            <mesh position={[0, 0, 0.008]}>
                <dodecahedronGeometry args={[0.02, 0]} />
                <meshBasicMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.flowerWhite} />
            </mesh>
        </group>
    </group>
);

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * VARIATION A: GRAND MEMORY PORTAL (Pedestals.jpg — Left Reference)
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const GrandMemoryPortal: React.FC<PedestalModelProps> = React.memo(
    ({ isNear, isEchoing, animRefs }) => {
        // Broken Circular Stone Portal Arch Segments (Left & Right Arms)
        const archSegments = useMemo(() => {
            const segs: Array<{
                pos: [number, number, number];
                rotZ: number;
                scale: [number, number, number];
                isInnerGlow?: boolean;
            }> = [];
            const radius = 1.38;

            // Left Arch Arm (from 195 deg up to 112 deg)
            for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
                const deg = 195 - i * 16.5;
                const rad = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
                segs.push({
                    pos: [Math.cos(rad) * radius, 2.15 + Math.sin(rad) * radius, -0.18],
                    rotZ: rad - Math.PI / 2,
                    scale: [0.42 - i * 0.015, 0.48, 0.44],
                });
            }

            // Right Arch Arm (from -15 deg up to 62 deg)
            for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
                const deg = -15 + i * 18;
                const rad = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
                segs.push({
                    pos: [Math.cos(rad) * radius, 2.15 + Math.sin(rad) * radius, -0.18],
                    rotZ: rad - Math.PI / 2,
                    scale: [0.4 - i * 0.015, 0.46, 0.42],
                });
            }

            return segs;
        }, []);

        const baseRocks = useMemo(
            () => [
                { pos: [-1.45, 0.18, 0.65] as [number, number, number], s: [0.42, 0.28, 0.38] as [number, number, number], seed: 11 },
                { pos: [1.48, 0.16, 0.55] as [number, number, number], s: [0.38, 0.25, 0.35] as [number, number, number], seed: 22 },
                { pos: [0.85, 0.15, 1.35] as [number, number, number], s: [0.34, 0.22, 0.32] as [number, number, number], seed: 33 },
                { pos: [-1.15, 0.22, -1.05] as [number, number, number], s: [0.45, 0.32, 0.4] as [number, number, number], seed: 44 },
                { pos: [1.22, 0.2, -0.95] as [number, number, number], s: [0.4, 0.28, 0.36] as [number, number, number], seed: 55 },
            ].map((r) => ({
                ...r,
                geo: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(1, 1, r.s, 0.2, r.seed, true),
            })),
            []
        );

        return (
            <group>
                {/* 1. Layered Circular Stepped Sandstone Flagstone Base */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.11, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[1.75, 1.92, 0.22, 10]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneDark} roughness={0.88} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 0.28, 0]} rotation={[0, 0.3, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[1.42, 1.58, 0.2, 9]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneMid} roughness={0.86} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 0.44, 0]} rotation={[0, 0.15, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[1.15, 1.26, 0.16, 8]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight} roughness={0.84} flatShading />
                </mesh>

                {/* Front Stepped Stone Stairs */}
                {[0, 1, 2].map((step) => (
                    <mesh
                        key={`stair-${step}`}
                        position={[0, 0.08 + step * 0.13, 1.68 - step * 0.26]}
                        castShadow
                        receiveShadow
                    >
                        <boxGeometry args={[0.92 - step * 0.08, 0.14, 0.36]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={step % 2 === 0 ? PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight : PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneMid}
                            roughness={0.85}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                ))}

                {/* 2. Hexagonal Sandstone Altar Body & Overhanging Top Cap */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.86, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.86, 0.98, 0.72, 6]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneDeep} roughness={0.86} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                {/* Sculpted Altar Corner Pillars */}
                {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((corner) => {
                    const a = (corner / 6) * Math.PI * 2 + Math.PI / 6;
                    return (
                        <mesh
                            key={`pil-${corner}`}
                            position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.86, 0.86, Math.sin(a) * 0.86]}
                            rotation={[0, -a, 0]}
                            castShadow
                        >
                            <boxGeometry args={[0.22, 0.74, 0.22]} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneMid} roughness={0.84} flatShading />
                        </mesh>
                    );
                })}

                {/* Broad Polygonal Altar Crown Slab */}
                <mesh position={[0, 1.28, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[1.08, 0.98, 0.22, 6]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight} roughness={0.82} flatShading />
                </mesh>

                {/* Front Carved Stone Relief Shield with 4-Point Golden Star Emblem */}
                <group position={[0, 0.96, 0.88]}>
                    <mesh castShadow>
                        <octahedronGeometry args={[0.26, 0]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight} roughness={0.82} flatShading />
                    </mesh>
                    {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((pt) => {
                        const a = (pt / 4) * Math.PI * 2;
                        return (
                            <mesh
                                key={pt}
                                position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.08, Math.sin(a) * 0.08, 0.14]}
                                rotation={[0, 0, a]}
                                scale={[0.55, 1.2, 0.4]}
                            >
                                <octahedronGeometry args={[0.075, 0]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial
                                    color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.emblemGold}
                                    emissive="#d98a2b"
                                    emissiveIntensity={0.45}
                                    flatShading
                                />
                            </mesh>
                        );
                    })}
                </group>

                {/* 3. Dual Hanging Orange-Red Banners (Front-Left & Front-Right) */}
                <HangingBannerWithEmblem
                    position={[-0.64, 1.22, 0.74]}
                    rotation={[-0.14, 0.48, 0.06]}
                />
                <HangingBannerWithEmblem
                    position={[0.64, 1.22, 0.74]}
                    rotation={[-0.14, -0.48, -0.06]}
                />

                {/* 4. Glowing Golden Basin on Altar Top */}
                <mesh
                    ref={animRefs?.wellGlowRef}
                    position={[0, 1.4, 0]}
                    rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
                >
                    <circleGeometry args={[0.62, 12]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalCore}
                        emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.wellGlow}
                        emissiveIntensity={isNear || isEchoing ? 2.4 : 1.4}
                    />
                </mesh>

                {/* 5. Broken Circular Stone Portal Arch Rising Behind Altar */}
                <group>
                    {archSegments.map((seg, idx) => (
                        <group
                            key={`arch-seg-${idx}`}
                            position={seg.pos}
                            rotation={[0, 0, seg.rotZ]}
                        >
                            <mesh castShadow receiveShadow>
                                <boxGeometry args={seg.scale} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial
                                    color={
                                        idx % 2 === 0
                                            ? PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight
                                            : PEDESTAL_PALETTE.terracottaStone
                                    }
                                    roughness={0.85}
                                    flatShading
                                />
                            </mesh>
                            {/* Recessed Golden Light Channel along Inner Arch Rim */}
                            <mesh position={[0, -seg.scale[1] * 0.48, 0.08]}>
                                <boxGeometry args={[seg.scale[0] * 0.92, 0.05, 0.12]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial
                                    color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.orbitGold}
                                    emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalMid}
                                    emissiveIntensity={1.5}
                                />
                            </mesh>
                        </group>
                    ))}
                </group>

                {/* 6. Arch Foliage, Ivy Vines & White Keepsake Blossoms */}
                {[
                    [-1.32, 2.75, -0.05],
                    [-1.42, 2.35, 0.02],
                    [-1.12, 3.15, -0.05],
                    [1.35, 2.45, -0.02],
                    [1.22, 1.85, 0.04],
                ].map(([vx, vy, vz], i) => (
                    <mesh
                        key={`arch-leaf-${i}`}
                        position={[vx, vy, vz]}
                        rotation={[0.2, i * 0.8, i * 0.5]}
                        scale={[1.2, 0.5, 0.7]}
                        castShadow
                    >
                        <octahedronGeometry args={[0.18, 0]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={i % 2 === 0 ? PEDESTAL_PALETTE.vineGreen : PEDESTAL_PALETTE.vineLight}
                            roughness={0.82}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                ))}
                <PedestalFlowerCluster position={[-1.22, 2.88, 0.08]} rotation={[0.4, 0.3, 0]} scale={1.15} />
                <PedestalFlowerCluster position={[1.25, 1.92, 0.1]} rotation={[0.4, -0.3, 0]} scale={1.0} />
                <PedestalFlowerCluster position={[-1.08, 0.38, 1.18]} rotation={[0.2, 0.2, 0]} scale={0.95} />
                <PedestalFlowerCluster position={[0.72, 0.52, 0.95]} rotation={[0.2, -0.2, 0]} scale={0.85} />

                {/* Base Rocks & Grass Tufts */}
                {baseRocks.map((r, i) => (
                    <mesh key={`b-rock-${i}`} geometry={r.geo} position={r.pos} castShadow receiveShadow>
                        <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneDark} roughness={0.88} flatShading />
                    </mesh>
                ))}

                {/* 7. Central Floating Golden-Orange Crystal & Orbit Ring */}
                <FloatingMemoryCrystalAssembly
                    position={[0, 2.25, 0]}
                    hasOrbitRing={true}
                    isNear={isNear}
                    isEchoing={isEchoing}
                    animRefs={animRefs}
                />
            </group>
        );
    }
);

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * VARIATION B: MINIMAL MEMORY PEDESTAL (Pedestals.jpg — Center Reference)
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const MinimalMemoryPedestal: React.FC<PedestalModelProps> = React.memo(
    ({ isNear, isEchoing, animRefs }) => {
        const cornerStones = useMemo(
            () => [
                { pos: [-0.72, 0.14, 0.42] as [number, number, number], s: [0.28, 0.22, 0.26] as [number, number, number], seed: 71 },
                { pos: [0.68, 0.12, 0.38] as [number, number, number], s: [0.24, 0.18, 0.22] as [number, number, number], seed: 82 },
            ].map((r) => ({
                ...r,
                geo: createSculptedFacetedGeometry(1, 1, r.s, 0.18, r.seed, true),
            })),
            []
        );

        return (
            <group>
                {/* 1. Squat Tapered Polygonal Sandstone Base */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.42, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.68, 0.88, 0.84, 7]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneMid}
                        roughness={0.86}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>

                {/* 2. Broad Layered Overhanging Polygonal Top Slab */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.92, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.92, 0.78, 0.24, 7]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight}
                        roughness={0.82}
                        flatShading
                    />
                </mesh>

                {/* 3. Circular Golden Ring Symbol on Top Surface */}
                <mesh
                    ref={animRefs?.wellGlowRef}
                    position={[0, 1.05, 0]}
                    rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
                >
                    <ringGeometry args={[0.36, 0.52, 24]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.orbitGold}
                        emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.wellGlow}
                        emissiveIntensity={isNear || isEchoing ? 2.2 : 1.25}
                        side={THREE.DoubleSide}
                    />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 1.045, 0]} rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}>
                    <circleGeometry args={[0.35, 16]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalCore}
                        emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalMid}
                        emissiveIntensity={isNear || isEchoing ? 1.8 : 0.85}
                    />
                </mesh>

                {/* 4. Single Prominent Orange-Red Banner on Front-Right */}
                <HangingBannerWithEmblem
                    position={[0.24, 0.92, 0.76]}
                    rotation={[-0.08, 0.22, 0]}
                />

                {/* 5. Base Stones & Minimal Leaf Tufts */}
                {cornerStones.map((r, i) => (
                    <mesh key={i} geometry={r.geo} position={r.pos} castShadow receiveShadow>
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneDark}
                            roughness={0.88}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                ))}
                {[-0.82, 0.78].map((lx, idx) => (
                    <mesh
                        key={`tuft-${idx}`}
                        position={[lx, 0.18, 0.25]}
                        rotation={[0.1, idx * 0.5, idx === 0 ? 0.35 : -0.35]}
                    >
                        <coneGeometry args={[0.12, 0.38, 4]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.vineGreen}
                            roughness={0.85}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                ))}

                {/* 6. Floating Golden-Orange Crystal above Pedestal */}
                <FloatingMemoryCrystalAssembly
                    position={[0, 1.82, 0]}
                    hasOrbitRing={false}
                    isNear={isNear}
                    isEchoing={isEchoing}
                    animRefs={animRefs}
                />
            </group>
        );
    }
);

/**
 * ============================================================================
 * VARIATION C: ARCHED MEMORY PEDESTAL (Pedestals.jpg — Right Reference)
 * ============================================================================
 */
export const ArchedMemoryPedestal: React.FC<PedestalModelProps> = React.memo(
    ({ isNear, isEchoing, animRefs }) => {
        // Partial Curved Stone Arch Arm on Left Side
        const archArmBlocks = useMemo(() => {
            const list: Array<{
                pos: [number, number, number];
                rotZ: number;
                scale: [number, number, number];
            }> = [];
            const radius = 1.12;
            for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
                const deg = 200 - i * 19;
                const rad = (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
                list.push({
                    pos: [Math.cos(rad) * radius + 0.12, 1.92 + Math.sin(rad) * radius, -0.08],
                    rotZ: rad - Math.PI / 2,
                    scale: [0.36 - i * 0.012, 0.44, 0.38],
                });
            }
            return list;
        }, []);

        return (
            <group>
                {/* 1. Stepped Sandstone Base */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.12, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[1.18, 1.32, 0.24, 8]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneDark} roughness={0.88} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 0.3, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.98, 1.08, 0.18, 8]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneMid} roughness={0.86} flatShading />
                </mesh>

                {/* 2. Medium Tapered Altar Column & Bevelled Top Slab */}
                <mesh position={[0, 0.72, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.76, 0.88, 0.68, 7]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneDeep} roughness={0.86} flatShading />
                </mesh>
                <mesh position={[0, 1.14, 0]} castShadow receiveShadow>
                    <cylinderGeometry args={[0.96, 0.84, 0.22, 7]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight} roughness={0.82} flatShading />
                </mesh>

                {/* 3. Carved 4-Petal Golden Relief Symbol on Front-Right Altar Face */}
                <group position={[0.26, 0.72, 0.78]} rotation={[0, 0.32, 0]}>
                    {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((p) => {
                        const a = (p / 4) * Math.PI * 2;
                        return (
                            <mesh
                                key={p}
                                position={[Math.cos(a) * 0.065, Math.sin(a) * 0.065, 0]}
                                rotation={[0, 0, a + Math.PI / 4]}
                                scale={[1, 0.55, 0.35]}
                            >
                                <octahedronGeometry args={[0.05, 0]} />
                                <meshStandardMaterial
                                    color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.emblemGold}
                                    emissive="#d98a2b"
                                    emissiveIntensity={0.4}
                                    flatShading
                                />
                            </mesh>
                        );
                    })}
                </group>

                {/* 4. Prominent Orange-Red Hanging Banner on Front-Left */}
                <HangingBannerWithEmblem
                    position={[-0.36, 1.08, 0.74]}
                    rotation={[-0.1, -0.28, 0.04]}
                />

                {/* 5. Warm Glowing Centre Basin Beneath Crystal */}
                <mesh
                    ref={animRefs?.wellGlowRef}
                    position={[0, 1.26, 0]}
                    rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
                >
                    <circleGeometry args={[0.56, 12]} />
                    <meshStandardMaterial
                        color={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.crystalCore}
                        emissive={PEDESTAL_PALETTE.wellGlow}
                        emissiveIntensity={isNear || isEchoing ? 2.4 : 1.45}
                    />
                </mesh>

                {/* 6. Partial Curved Stone Arch Arm Rising from Left */}
                <group>
                    {archArmBlocks.map((b, idx) => (
                        <mesh
                            key={`c-arch-${idx}`}
                            position={b.pos}
                            rotation={[0, 0, b.rotZ]}
                            castShadow
                            receiveShadow
                        >
                            <boxGeometry args={b.scale} />
                            <meshStandardMaterial
                                color={
                                    idx % 2 === 0
                                        ? PEDESTAL_PALETTE.sandstoneLight
                                        : PEDESTAL_PALETTE.terracottaStone
                                }
                                roughness={0.85}
                                flatShading
                            />
                        </mesh>
                    ))}
                </group>

                {/* 7. Integrated Vine Leaves & White Blossom on Arch Arm */}
                {[
                    [-0.88, 2.32, 0.08],
                    [-0.68, 2.68, 0.06],
                    [-0.95, 1.85, 0.05],
                ].map(([vx, vy, vz], i) => (
                    <mesh
                        key={`c-leaf-${i}`}
                        position={[vx, vy, vz]}
                        rotation={[0.2, i * 0.6, 0.4]}
                        scale={[1.2, 0.5, 0.7]}
                        castShadow
                    >
                        <octahedronGeometry args={[0.17, 0]} />
                        <meshStandardMaterial
                            color={i % 2 === 0 ? PEDESTAL_PALETTE.vineGreen : PEDESTAL_PALETTE.vineLight}
                            roughness={0.82}
                            flatShading
                        />
                    </mesh>
                ))}
                <PedestalFlowerCluster
                    position={[-0.74, 2.48, 0.14]}
                    rotation={[0.35, 0.25, 0]}
                    scale={1.1}
                />

                {/* 8. Floating Golden-Orange Crystal with Thin Golden Orbit Ring */}
                <FloatingMemoryCrystalAssembly
                    position={[0.05, 1.98, 0]}
                    hasOrbitRing={true}
                    ringTilt={[0.32, -0.18, 0.28]}
                    isNear={isNear}
                    isEchoing={isEchoing}
                    animRefs={animRefs}
                />
            </group>
        );
    }
);

END OF FILE: src/components/Memory/PedestalModels.tsx

# GROUP 9: USER INTERFACE (HUD, MODALS & CUSTOM CURSOR)
# Diegetic parchment exploration HUD, memory detail cards, compass minimap, and custom botanical pointer.

================================================================================
FILE #33 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/components/UI/GameHUD.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\components\UI\GameHUD.tsx
FILE TYPE     : .tsx
DESCRIPTION   : Parchment-themed exploration HUD with memory counter, compass, memory journal modal, and interaction prompt.

// src/components/UI/GameHUD.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import {
    BookOpen,
    UserCheck,
    Settings,
    Sparkles,
    X,
    MapPin,
    Calendar,
    Volume2,
    Sprout,
    Compass,
    Layers,
    Navigation,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { playerTelemetry, useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import { getGardenTerrainHeight } from '../../scenes/Environment/lowPolyUtils';
import {
    KEEPSAKE_ASSET_MANIFEST,
    type AssetManifestEntry,
} from '../../systems/AssetManager/glbExporter';
import './GameHUD.css';

export const GameHUD: React.FC = () => {
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const inputMode = useGameStore((state) => state.inputMode);
    const setInputMode = useGameStore((state) => state.setInputMode);
    const toggleInputMode = useGameStore((state) => state.toggleInputMode);
    const nearbyMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.nearbyMemory);
    const activeMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.activeMemory);
    const persistentMemories = useGameStore((state) => state.persistentMemories);
    const openMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.openMemory);
    const closeMemory = useGameStore((state) => state.closeMemory);
    const advanceMemoryGrowth = useGameStore((state) => state.advanceMemoryGrowth);
    const discoveredIds = useGameStore((state) => state.discoveredIds);
    const characterModel = useGameStore((state) => state.characterModel);
    const toggleCharacterModel = useGameStore(
        (state) => state.toggleCharacterModel
    );
    const nearbyZone = useGameStore((state) => state.nearbyZone);
    const activeChunkCoord = useGameStore((state) => state.activeChunkCoord);
    const setPhase = useGameStore((state) => state.setPhase);
    const setIntroStage = useGameStore((state) => state.setIntroStage);

    const [showAssetCatalog, setShowAssetCatalog] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (
                e.code === 'KeyC' &&
                phase === 'exploring' &&
                !useGameStore.getState().activeMemory
            ) {
                toggleCharacterModel();
            }
        };
        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
        return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    }, [phase, toggleCharacterModel]);

    if (phase !== 'exploring') return null;

    const currentMemoryData = activeMemory
        ? persistentMemories.find((m) => m.id === activeMemory.id) || activeMemory
        : null;

    const handleReplayIntro = () => {
        closeMemory();
        setShowAssetCatalog(false);
        setIntroStage(1);
        setPhase('loading');
    };

    const handleTeleportToAsset = (entry: AssetManifestEntry) => {
        const [tx, tz] = entry.worldCoords;
        const ty = getGardenTerrainHeight(tx, tz);
        playerTelemetry.position = [tx, ty, tz];
        playerTelemetry.velocity = [0, 0, 0];
        setShowAssetCatalog(false);
        setInputMode('camera');
    };

    return (
        <div className="keepsake-hud-layer">
            {/* Top-Left Keepsake Brand Logo & World Status */}
            <div className="hud-top-left">
                <div
                    className="hud-brand-logo"
                    onClick={handleReplayIntro}
                    title="Click to replay brushstroke intro sequence"
                >
                    <span className="hud-brand-text">Keepsake</span>
                    <svg
                        className="hud-brand-leaf"
                        viewBox="0 0 32 32"
                        width="22"
                        height="22"
                    >
                        <path
                            d="M6,26 C10,12 20,6 30,4 C26,16 18,24 6,26 Z"
                            fill="#f7ecda"
                        />
                    </svg>
                </div>

                <div className="hud-pills-row">
                    <div className="hud-discovery-pill">
                        <span>
                            Memories Awoken: {discoveredIds.length} /{' '}
                            {persistentMemories.length}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="hud-discovery-pill hud-chunk-pill">
                        <Compass size={13} />
                        <span>
                            Chunk ({activeChunkCoord[0]}, {activeChunkCoord[1]})
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Top-Center Landmark Zone Banner */}
            {nearbyZone && !currentMemoryData && !showAssetCatalog && (
                <div className="hud-zone-banner">
                    <span className="hud-zone-type">
                        {nearbyZone.zoneType.replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <h3 className="hud-zone-title">{nearbyZone.name}</h3>
                    <p className="hud-zone-desc">{nearbyZone.description}</p>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Top-Right Action Bar */}
            <div className="hud-top-right">
                <button
                    className="hud-explorer-switch-pill"
                    onClick={toggleCharacterModel}
                    title="Switch Playable Explorer (Shortcut: C)"
                >
                    <UserCheck size={16} />
                    <span>
                        {characterModel === 'female'
                            ? 'Wanderer: Female (F)'
                            : 'Wanderer: Male (M)'}
                    </span>
                    <kbd className="hud-mini-key">C</kbd>
                </button>

                <button
                    className="hud-explorer-switch-pill"
                    onClick={() => {
                        setShowAssetCatalog((prev) => !prev);
                        setInputMode('cursor');
                    }}
                    title="Inspect 3D Asset Library in World"
                >
                    <Layers size={16} />
                    <span>3D Assets ({KEEPSAKE_ASSET_MANIFEST.length})</span>
                </button>

                <button
                    className="hud-circle-btn"
                    onClick={() =>
                        openMemory(nearbyMemory || persistentMemories[0])
                    }
                    title="Open Memory Journal"
                >
                    <BookOpen size={18} />
                </button>

                <button
                    className="hud-circle-btn"
                    onClick={handleReplayIntro}
                    title="Replay Brushstroke Loading Sequence"
                >
                    <Settings size={18} />
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* 3D Asset Library Quick-Inspector Modal */}
            {showAssetCatalog && (
                <div
                    className="memory-modal-backdrop"
                    onClick={() => setShowAssetCatalog(false)}
                >
                    <div
                        className="memory-parchment-card asset-catalog-card"
                        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    >
                        <button
                            className="memory-close-btn"
                            onClick={() => setShowAssetCatalog(false)}
                            aria-label="Close Asset Catalog"
                        >
                            <X size={20} />
                        </button>

                        <div className="memory-card-header">
                            <span className="memory-category-tag">
                                REAL-TIME 3D ASSET MANIFEST
                            </span>
                            <h2 className="memory-card-title">
                                Keepsake 3D World Library
                            </h2>
                            <p className="memory-card-subtitle">
                                Click any asset below to teleport your Explorer directly
                                to its location in the garden.
                            </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="asset-manifest-list">
                            {KEEPSAKE_ASSET_MANIFEST.map((item) => (
                                <div key={item.id} className="asset-manifest-row">
                                    <div className="asset-manifest-info">
                                        <div className="asset-manifest-title-row">
                                            <strong>{item.name}</strong>
                                            <span className="asset-manifest-dim">
                                                {item.dimensions}
                                            </span>
                                        </div>
                                        <span className="asset-manifest-path">
                                            {item.filename}
                                        </span>
                                        <p className="asset-manifest-desc">
                                            {item.intendedUse}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        className="asset-teleport-btn"
                                        onClick={() => handleTeleportToAsset(item)}
                                    >
                                        <Navigation size={14} />
                                        <span>Visit in 3D</span>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Contextual Proximity Prompt */}
            {nearbyMemory && !currentMemoryData && !showAssetCatalog && (
                <div className="hud-proximity-prompt">
                    <button
                        className="remember-prompt-pill"
                        onClick={() => openMemory(nearbyMemory)}
                    >
                        <span className="remember-prompt-text">Remember?</span>
                        <kbd className="remember-prompt-key">E</kbd>
                    </button>
                    <span className="remember-prompt-subtitle">
                        {nearbyMemory.title} ({nearbyMemory.subtitle})
                    </span>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Bottom-Left Controls */}
            <div className="hud-bottom-left-controls">
                <div className="control-row">
                    <div className="wasd-cluster">
                        <div className="wasd-top">
                            <kbd className="key-cap key-small">W</kbd>
                        </div>
                        <div className="wasd-bottom">
                            <kbd className="key-cap key-small">A</kbd>
                            <kbd className="key-cap key-small">S</kbd>
                            <kbd className="key-cap key-small">D</kbd>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="control-label">Move</span>
                </div>

                <div className="control-row">
                    <span className="mouse-icon-badge" />
                    <span className="control-label">
                        {inputMode === 'camera'
                            ? 'Mode A: Camera Look Active'
                            : 'Mode B: UI Cursor Active (Click World or Alt to Look)'}
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div className="control-row">
                    <kbd
                        className={`key-cap key-wide ${inputMode === 'cursor' ? 'key-cap--active' : ''
                            }`}
                        onClick={toggleInputMode}
                        style={{ cursor: 'pointer', pointerEvents: 'auto' }}
                    >
                        Alt
                    </kbd>
                    <span className="control-label">Toggle Camera / UI Cursor</span>
                </div>

                <div className="control-row">
                    <kbd className="key-cap">E</kbd>
                    <span className="control-label">Awaken Memory Pedestal</span>
                </div>

                <div className="control-row">
                    <kbd className="key-cap key-wide">Shift</kbd>
                    <span className="control-label">Sprint</span>
                    <kbd className="key-cap key-wide" style={{ marginLeft: 4 }}>
                        Space
                    </kbd>
                    <span className="control-label">Jump</span>
                </div>
            </div>

            {/* Bottom-Right "Remember" Button */}
            <div className="hud-bottom-right">
                <button
                    className="hud-remember-main-btn"
                    onClick={() =>
                        openMemory(nearbyMemory || persistentMemories[0])
                    }
                >
                    <Sparkles size={18} className="remember-flower-icon" />
                    <span>Remember</span>
                </button>
            </div>

            {/* Memory Modal */}
            {currentMemoryData && (
                <div className="memory-modal-backdrop" onClick={closeMemory}>
                    <div
                        className="memory-parchment-card"
                        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    >
                        <button
                            className="memory-close-btn"
                            onClick={closeMemory}
                            aria-label="Close Memory Echo"
                        >
                            <X size={20} />
                        </button>

                        <div className="memory-card-header">
                            <div className="memory-badges-row">
                                <span className="memory-category-tag">
                                    {currentMemoryData.category.toUpperCase()} ECHO
                                </span>
                                <span className="memory-stage-badge">
                                    <Sprout size={13} />
                                    {currentMemoryData.subtitle}
                                </span>
                                {currentMemoryData.importance && (
                                    <span className="memory-importance-badge">
                                        {currentMemoryData.importance.toUpperCase()}
                                    </span>
                                )}
                            </div>
                            <h2 className="memory-card-title">{currentMemoryData.title}</h2>
                            <p className="memory-card-subtitle">
                                {currentMemoryData.location}
                            </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="memory-visual-frame">
                            <div className="memory-visual-art">
                                <div className="memory-art-sun" />
                                <div className="memory-art-mountains" />
                                <div className="memory-art-meadow" />
                                {currentMemoryData.echo?.ambientAudioLabel && (
                                    <div className="memory-echo-audio-pill">
                                        <Volume2 size={14} />
                                        <span>{currentMemoryData.echo.ambientAudioLabel}</span>
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {currentMemoryData.echo?.voiceTranscript && (
                            <blockquote className="memory-voice-echo">
                                {currentMemoryData.echo.voiceTranscript}
                            </blockquote>
                        )}

                        <div className="memory-meta-row">
                            <span className="memory-meta-item">
                                <Calendar size={14} /> {currentMemoryData.date}
                            </span>
                            <span className="memory-meta-item">
                                <MapPin size={14} /> {currentMemoryData.location}
                            </span>
                            {currentMemoryData.people && (
                                <span className="memory-meta-item">
                                    With: {currentMemoryData.people.join(', ')}
                                </span>
                            )}
                        </div>

                        <p className="memory-card-description">
                            {currentMemoryData.description}
                        </p>

                        <div className="memory-tag-list">
                            {currentMemoryData.tags.map((tag) => (
                                <span key={tag} className="memory-tag-chip">
                                    #{tag}
                                </span>
                            ))}
                        </div>

                        <div className="memory-card-footer">
                            {currentMemoryData.growthStage !== 'landmark' && (
                                <button
                                    className="memory-nourish-btn"
                                    onClick={() => advanceMemoryGrowth(currentMemoryData.id)}
                                >
                                    <Sprout size={16} />
                                    <span>Nourish Memory Growth</span>
                                </button>
                            )}
                            <button className="memory-return-btn" onClick={closeMemory}>
                                Return to the Garden
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

FILE #35 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/components/UI/CustomCursor.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\components\UI\CustomCursor.tsx
FILE TYPE     : .tsx
DESCRIPTION   : Custom animated botanical SVG mouse pointer with spring interpolation and click reactions.
================================================================================
// src/components/UI/CustomCursor.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useGameStore } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import './CustomCursor.css';

export const CustomCursor: React.FC = () => {
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const inputMode = useGameStore((state) => state.inputMode);
    const activeModal = useGameStore((state) => state.activeModal);

    const cursorRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    // Custom botanical cursor is only active in Cursor Mode or during UI modals / loading
    const showBotanicalCursor =
        phase !== 'exploring' || inputMode === 'cursor' || activeModal !== 'none';

    useEffect(() => {
        const onMouseMove = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            if (cursorRef.current) {
                cursorRef.current.style.transform = `translate3d(${e.clientX}px, ${e.clientY}px, 0)`;
            }
        };

        window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove, { passive: true });
        return () => window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (showBotanicalCursor) {
            document.body.classList.add('keepsake-custom-cursor-active');
        } else {
            document.body.classList.remove('keepsake-custom-cursor-active');
        }
    }, [showBotanicalCursor]);

    if (!showBotanicalCursor) return null;

    return (
        <div
            ref={cursorRef}
            className={`keepsake-botanical-cursor ${inputMode === 'cursor' ? 'keepsake-botanical-cursor--alt-active' : ''
                }`}
            aria-hidden="true"
        >
            <svg
                width="34"
                height="34"
                viewBox="0 0 36 36"
                fill="none"
                xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
            >
                <defs>
                    <filter id="cursorWarmGlow" x="-30%" y="-30%" width="160%" height="160%">
                        <feDropShadow
                            dx="0"
                            dy="2"
                            stdDeviation="2.5"
                            floodColor="#2b1b16"
                            floodOpacity="0.55"
                        />
                    </filter>
                </defs>
                <g filter="url(#cursorWarmGlow)">
                    <path
                        d="M 3,3 C 12,4 22,9 26,18 C 17,22 10,16 3,3 Z"
                        fill="#f7ecda"
                        stroke="#c69258"
                        strokeWidth="1.4"
                    />
                    <path
                        d="M 4,4 Q 14,11 22,16"
                        stroke="#c69258"
                        strokeWidth="1.2"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                    />
                    <path
                        d="M 12,18 C 11,24 14,29 20,30 C 20,24 17,20 12,18 Z"
                        fill="#e8b882"
                        stroke="#b58046"
                        strokeWidth="1"
                    />
                    <circle cx="3.5" cy="3.5" r="2.2" fill="#ffd87d" />
                </g>
            </svg>
            {inputMode === 'cursor' && (
                <span className="cursor-mode-tag">UI Mode [Alt]</span>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

================================================================================
FILE #37 OF 40
RELATIVE PATH : src/components/LoadingScreen/LoadingScreen.tsx
ABSOLUTE PATH : D:\Visual Studio Codes\Projects\Keepsake\src\components\LoadingScreen\LoadingScreen.tsx
FILE TYPE     : .tsx
DESCRIPTION   : Intro loading screen with animated brushstrokes, poetic narrative prologue, and enter button.

// src/components/LoadingScreen/LoadingScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useGameStore, type IntroStage } from '../../store/useGameStore';
import './LoadingScreen.css';

interface GuideStrokeSpec {
    id: string;
    d: string;
    strokeWidth: number;
    weight: number;
}

const CALLIGRAPHY_GUIDE_STROKES: GuideStrokeSpec[] = [
    { id: 'k-stem', d: 'M 225,46 C 204,88 180,142 162,196', strokeWidth: 48, weight: 0.11 },
    { id: 'k-upper', d: 'M 292,54 C 258,74 220,104 188,124', strokeWidth: 46, weight: 0.09 },
    { id: 'k-lower', d: 'M 192,122 C 218,142 246,162 278,150', strokeWidth: 44, weight: 0.08 },
    { id: 'ee-loop', d: 'M 282,138 C 306,110 330,106 318,136 C 304,160 342,154 364,132 C 384,108 398,116 382,144 C 370,162 404,158 422,138', strokeWidth: 56, weight: 0.16 },
    { id: 'p-stroke', d: 'M 426,122 C 414,158 400,196 392,222 C 406,178 426,118 456,120 C 480,124 472,158 438,154', strokeWidth: 54, weight: 0.13 },
    { id: 'sa-stroke', d: 'M 474,146 C 498,116 526,124 514,152 C 504,166 544,154 568,126 C 594,108 604,132 586,154 C 596,158 614,150 626,136', strokeWidth: 58, weight: 0.15 },
    { id: 'ke-stroke', d: 'M 634,68 C 620,108 606,144 598,162 C 618,134 652,114 662,130 C 646,146 632,148 656,156 C 682,158 712,126 696,116 C 676,114 674,156 718,144', strokeWidth: 58, weight: 0.14 },
    { id: 'underline-flourish', d: 'M 218,138 C 285,198 375,212 492,188 C 565,173 638,168 692,182', strokeWidth: 30, weight: 0.09 },
    { id: 'leaf-motif', d: 'M 708,86 C 722,68 742,50 764,42', strokeWidth: 50, weight: 0.05 },
];

export const LoadingScreen: React.FC = () => {
    const phase = useGameStore((state) => state.phase);
    const setPhase = useGameStore((state) => state.setPhase);
    const introStage = useGameStore((state) => state.introStage);
    const setIntroStage = useGameStore((state) => state.setIntroStage);
    const characterModel = useGameStore((state) => state.characterModel);
    const setCharacterModel = useGameStore((state) => state.setCharacterModel);
    const isInitialTerrainReady = useGameStore((state) => state.isInitialTerrainReady);

    const maskPathRefs = useRef<Array<SVGPathElement | null>>([]);
    const underlineVisRef = useRef<SVGPathElement | null>(null);
    const brushFollowerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

    const [paintComplete, setPaintComplete] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (phase !== 'loading') return;

        setPaintComplete(false);
        setIntroStage(1);

        let rafId = 0;
        let startTime = 0;
        const PAINT_DURATION_MS = 3200;

        const startTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            setIntroStage(2);

            const pathMetrics = CALLIGRAPHY_GUIDE_STROKES.map((spec, idx) => {
                const el = maskPathRefs.current[idx];
                const len = el ? el.getTotalLength() : 200;
                if (el) {
                    el.style.strokeDasharray = `${len}`;
                    el.style.strokeDashoffset = `${len}`;
                }
                return { el, len, weight: spec.weight };
            });

            const underlineEl = underlineVisRef.current;
            const underlineLen = underlineEl ? underlineEl.getTotalLength() : 480;
            if (underlineEl) {
                underlineEl.style.strokeDasharray = `${underlineLen}`;
                underlineEl.style.strokeDashoffset = `${underlineLen}`;
            }

            let smoothX = 225;
            let smoothY = 46;
            let smoothAngle = -14;

            const animateBrush = (now: number) => {
                if (!startTime) startTime = now;
                const elapsed = now - startTime;
                const rawProgress = Math.min(1, elapsed / PAINT_DURATION_MS);
                const globalT = 0.5 - 0.5 * Math.cos(rawProgress * Math.PI);

                let accumulatedWeight = 0;
                let targetX = smoothX;
                let targetY = smoothY;
                let targetAngle = smoothAngle;

                for (let i = 0; i < pathMetrics.length; i++) {
                    const m = pathMetrics[i];
                    const segStart = accumulatedWeight;
                    const segEnd = accumulatedWeight + m.weight;
                    accumulatedWeight = segEnd;

                    if (!m.el) continue;

                    if (globalT >= segEnd) {
                        m.el.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';
                        if (i === 7 && underlineEl) {
                            underlineEl.style.strokeDashoffset = '0';
                        }
                    } else if (globalT <= segStart) {
                        m.el.style.strokeDashoffset = `${m.len}`;
                    } else {
                        const localRaw = (globalT - segStart) / m.weight;
                        const localEased = localRaw * localRaw * (3 - 2 * localRaw);
                        const drawDist = localEased * m.len;

                        m.el.style.strokeDashoffset = `${m.len - drawDist}`;

                        if (i === 7 && underlineEl) {
                            underlineEl.style.strokeDashoffset = `${underlineLen * (1 - localEased)
                                }`;
                        }

                        const pt = m.el.getPointAtLength(drawDist);
                        const aheadPt = m.el.getPointAtLength(
                            Math.min(m.len, drawDist + 4)
                        );
                        targetX = pt.x;
                        targetY = pt.y;

                        const dx = aheadPt.x - pt.x;
                        const dy = aheadPt.y - pt.y;
                        if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 0.05) {
                            const strokeDeg = (Math.atan2(dy, dx) * 180) / Math.PI;
                            targetAngle = Math.max(-28, Math.min(8, strokeDeg * 0.22 - 14));
                        }
                    }
                }

                smoothX += (targetX - smoothX) * 0.36;
                smoothY += (targetY - smoothY) * 0.36;
                smoothAngle += (targetAngle - smoothAngle) * 0.26;

                if (brushFollowerRef.current) {
                    const pctX = (smoothX / 920) * 100;
                    const pctY = (smoothY / 250) * 100;
                    const opacity =
                        rawProgress < 0.05
                            ? rawProgress / 0.05
                            : rawProgress > 0.94
                                ? (1 - rawProgress) / 0.06
                                : 1;

                    brushFollowerRef.current.style.left = `${pctX}%`;
                    brushFollowerRef.current.style.top = `${pctY}%`;
                    brushFollowerRef.current.style.transform = `translate(-4%, -64%) rotate(${smoothAngle.toFixed(
                        2
                    )}deg)`;
                    brushFollowerRef.current.style.opacity = `${opacity}`;
                }

                if (rawProgress < 1) {
                    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animateBrush);
                } else {
                    setPaintComplete(true);
                    setIntroStage(3);
                }
            };

            rafId = requestAnimationFrame(animateBrush);
        }, 550);

        const stage4Timer = setTimeout(() => {
            setIntroStage(4);
        }, 4350);

        return () => {
            clearTimeout(startTimer);
            clearTimeout(stage4Timer);
            cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
        };
    }, [phase, setIntroStage]);

    const handleEnterGarden = () => {
        if (introStage >= 5) return;
        setIntroStage(5 as IntroStage);

        setTimeout(() => {
            setIntroStage(6 as IntroStage);
        }, 2000);

        setTimeout(() => {
            // Transition only into gameplay when initial terrain is generated
            setPhase('exploring');
        }, 2750);
    };

    if (phase === 'exploring') return null;

    return (
        <div
            className={`keepsake-loading-overlay ${introStage === 6 ? 'keepsake-loading-overlay--fade-out' : ''
                }`}
        >
            <div
                className={`loading-atmosphere-veil ${introStage <= 2 ? 'loading-atmosphere-veil--misty' : ''
                    }`}
            />

            <div className="loading-content-frame">
                <div
                    className={`brand-header-group ${introStage >= 2 ? 'brand-header-group--visible' : ''
                        }`}
                >
                    <div className="logo-svg-stage">
                        <svg
                            className="keepsake-logo-svg"
                            viewBox="0 0 920 250"
                            preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
                            xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
                        >
                            <defs>
                                <filter id="bristleMaskEdge" x="-10%" y="-10%" width="120%" height="120%">
                                    <feTurbulence
                                        type="fractalNoise"
                                        baseFrequency="0.065"
                                        numOctaves="2"
                                        result="bristleNoise"
                                    />
                                    <feDisplacementMap
                                        in="SourceGraphic"
                                        in2="bristleNoise"
                                        scale="7"
                                        xChannelSelector="R"
                                        yChannelSelector="G"
                                    />
                                </filter>

                                <mask id="calligraphyContourMask">
                                    <g filter="url(#bristleMaskEdge)">
                                        {CALLIGRAPHY_GUIDE_STROKES.map((spec, idx) => (
                                            <path
                                                key={spec.id}
                                                ref={(el) => {
                                                    maskPathRefs.current[idx] = el;
                                                }}
                                                d={spec.d}
                                                fill="none"
                                                stroke="#ffffff"
                                                strokeWidth={spec.strokeWidth}
                                                strokeLinecap="round"
                                                strokeLinejoin="round"
                                            />
                                        ))}
                                    </g>
                                    {paintComplete && (
                                        <rect x="0" y="0" width="920" height="250" fill="#ffffff" />
                                    )}
                                </mask>

                                <filter
                                    id="logoSoftShadow"
                                    x="-15%"
                                    y="-15%"
                                    width="130%"
                                    height="130%"
                                >
                                    <feDropShadow
                                        dx="0"
                                        dy="4"
                                        stdDeviation="6"
                                        floodColor="#3b2422"
                                        floodOpacity="0.46"
                                    />
                                </filter>
                            </defs>

                            <g
                                mask="url(#calligraphyContourMask)"
                                filter="url(#logoSoftShadow)"
                            >
                                <text
                                    x="452"
                                    y="152"
                                    textAnchor="middle"
                                    className="keepsake-svg-wordmark"
                                >
                                    Keepsake
                                </text>

                                <path
                                    ref={underlineVisRef}
                                    d="M 218,136 C 285,198 375,212 492,188 C 565,173 638,168 690,182"
                                    fill="none"
                                    stroke="#f7ecda"
                                    strokeWidth="5.5"
                                    strokeLinecap="round"
                                />
                                <path
                                    d="M 245,155 C 310,202 385,208 492,186 C 555,174 615,170 665,178"
                                    fill="none"
                                    stroke="#f7ecda"
                                    strokeWidth="2.4"
                                    strokeLinecap="round"
                                    opacity="0.82"
                                />

                                <g transform="translate(715, 58) rotate(-8)">
                                    <path
                                        d="M -6,28 Q 8,14 26,-2"
                                        stroke="#f7ecda"
                                        strokeWidth="2.6"
                                        strokeLinecap="round"
                                        fill="none"
                                    />
                                    <path
                                        d="M 6,16 C 10,-2 28,-12 46,-14 C 40,6 26,20 6,16 Z"
                                        fill="#f7ecda"
                                    />
                                    <path
                                        d="M -4,20 C -10,8 -4,-4 8,-8 C 8,4 4,14 -4,20 Z"
                                        fill="#f2dfc4"
                                    />
                                </g>
                            </g>
                        </svg>

                        {/* Moving Artist Paintbrush Follower (Cleaned: no translucent white circle) */}
                        {introStage === 2 && (
                            <div
                                ref={brushFollowerRef}
                                className="artist-paintbrush-follower"
                                aria-hidden="true"
                            >
                                <svg viewBox="0 0 220 70" width="195" height="62">
                                    <defs>
                                        <linearGradient id="woodHandle" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
                                            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#9e643c" />
                                            <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#b87b4f" />
                                            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#784726" />
                                        </linearGradient>
                                        <linearGradient id="brassFerrule" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                                            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#f5dfa6" />
                                            <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#c9a563" />
                                            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#8c6c38" />
                                        </linearGradient>
                                    </defs>
                                    <path d="M 78,28 L 216,2 L 220,15 L 82,40 Z" fill="url(#woodHandle)" />
                                    <path d="M 48,33 L 79,27 L 83,41 L 52,46 Z" fill="url(#brassFerrule)" />
                                    <path d="M 4,44 C 18,30 34,31 49,33 L 52,46 C 35,51 18,50 4,44 Z" fill="#4c3832" />
                                    <path d="M 9,41 L 46,35 M 11,44 L 48,39 M 13,47 L 47,43" stroke="#6e5248" strokeWidth="0.9" />
                                    <path d="M 3,44 C 11,38 20,38 26,40 C 20,46 11,47 3,44 Z" fill="#f7ecda" />
                                </svg>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div
                        className={`subtitle-ornament-group ${introStage >= 3 ? 'subtitle-ornament-group--visible' : ''
                            }`}
                    >
                        <p className="keepsake-spaced-subtitle">
                            A WORLD PAINTED FROM MEMORIES
                        </p>

                        <div className="botanical-divider-row">
                            <span className="ornament-line" />
                            <svg className="ornament-sprig-svg" viewBox="0 0 90 28" width="68" height="22">
                                <path d="M 14,16 Q 45,12 76,13" stroke="#f7ecda" strokeWidth="1.6" fill="none" strokeLinecap="round" />
                                <path d="M 30,14 C 24,7 16,6 12,10 C 17,15 24,16 30,14 Z" fill="#f7ecda" />
                                <path d="M 46,13 C 42,5 35,3 29,6 C 33,12 40,14 46,13 Z" fill="#f7ecda" />
                                <path d="M 42,15 C 38,22 30,24 24,20 C 29,16 36,14 42,15 Z" fill="#f7ecda" />
                                <path d="M 60,13 C 64,6 72,5 78,9 C 72,14 65,15 60,13 Z" fill="#f7ecda" />
                                <path d="M 56,14 C 60,20 67,22 73,19 C 68,15 62,14 56,14 Z" fill="#f7ecda" />
                            </svg>
                            <span className="ornament-line" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="loading-lower-slot">
                    {introStage === 4 && (
                        <div className="intro-interactive-stack">
                            <div className="explorer-select-row">
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    className={`explorer-choice-pill ${characterModel === 'female' ? 'active' : ''
                                        }`}
                                    onClick={() => setCharacterModel('female')}
                                >
                                    <span className="explorer-choice-dot" />
                                    <span>Female Explorer (Floral Orb & Headphones)</span>
                                </button>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    className={`explorer-choice-pill ${characterModel === 'male' ? 'active' : ''
                                        }`}
                                    onClick={() => setCharacterModel('male')}
                                >
                                    <span className="explorer-choice-dot" />
                                    <span>Male Explorer (Brass Lantern & Goggles)</span>
                                </button>
                            </div>

                            <button
                                type="button"
                                className="sculpted-stone-enter-btn"
                                onClick={handleEnterGarden}
                            >
                                <svg className="stone-btn-svg" viewBox="0 0 340 86" width="310" height="78">
                                    <defs>
                                        <linearGradient id="stoneRimGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                                            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#d9c0a6" />
                                            <stop offset="55%" stopColor="#b3967d" />
                                            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#856d5c" />
                                        </linearGradient>
                                        <linearGradient id="parchmentFaceGrad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                                            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#f3e5d2" />
                                            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#dfc8ae" />
                                        </linearGradient>
                                    </defs>
                                    <polygon
                                        points="28,8 95,4 245,4 312,8 334,26 336,58 310,78 240,82 100,82 26,78 4,58 6,26"
                                        fill="url(#stoneRimGrad)"
                                        stroke="#f7e8d4"
                                        strokeWidth="1"
                                        strokeOpacity="0.6"
                                    />
                                    <polygon
                                        points="34,15 100,12 240,12 306,15 324,29 325,54 304,69 238,72 102,72 34,69 15,54 16,29"
                                        fill="url(#parchmentFaceGrad)"
                                    />
                                </svg>
                                <span className="stone-btn-label">
                                    {isInitialTerrainReady ? 'Enter the Garden' : 'Preparing the Garden...'}
                                </span>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {introStage >= 5 && (
                        <div className="circular-brush-loader-group">
                            <div className="circular-brush-ring-box">
                                <svg className="circular-brush-svg" viewBox="0 0 160 160" width="126" height="126">
                                    <defs>
                                        <linearGradient id="ringWarmGlow" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
                                            <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#fff9eb" stopOpacity="0.95" />
                                            <stop offset="65%" stopColor="#ffe3b3" stopOpacity="0.9" />
                                            <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#ffbe6b" stopOpacity="0.2" />
                                        </linearGradient>
                                        <filter id="ringBloom" x="-25%" y="-25%" width="150%" height="150%">
                                            <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="3.5" result="blur" />
                                            <feMerge>
                                                <feMergeNode in="blur" />
                                                <feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic" />
                                            </feMerge>
                                        </filter>
                                    </defs>

                                    <g className="rotating-enso-group" filter="url(#ringBloom)">
                                        <path
                                            d="M 126,64 C 118,32 84,18 52,29 C 24,39 14,72 26,102 C 38,130 74,142 104,126 C 118,118 127,104 130,90"
                                            fill="none"
                                            stroke="url(#ringWarmGlow)"
                                            strokeWidth="5.2"
                                            strokeLinecap="round"
                                        />
                                        <path
                                            d="M 66,133 C 88,136 112,124 124,102"
                                            fill="none"
                                            stroke="#ffe4a8"
                                            strokeWidth="6.5"
                                            strokeLinecap="round"
                                        />
                                    </g>

                                    <g fill="#fff4de" filter="url(#ringBloom)" transform="translate(128, 86) rotate(-18)">
                                        <path d="M 0,0 C 4,-8 12,-11 18,-9 C 14,-2 7,2 0,0 Z" />
                                        <path d="M -2,6 C -7,0 -7,-8 -2,-13 C 2,-7 2,0 -2,6 Z" />
                                    </g>
                                </svg>
                            </div>
                            <p className="entering-garden-caption">Entering the Garden...</p>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};
